</details>


### [97] [MMAP: Multimodal Missing-Aware Pretraining for Longitudinal Alzheimer's Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26617)
*Fiona Kekwick,Matthew Baugh,Bernhard Kainz,Paul M. Matthews,Wenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MMAP，一种用于从不完整数据中学习图像-表格表示的新型多模态缺失感知对齐预训练方法。该方法通过高效的Sigmoid对比学习与生成重建相结合的方式预训练图像编码器，并基于表格基础模型构建表格编码器。缺失标记生成器使两个编码器能够处理不完整的数据，从而增强模型对缺失模态（如缺失图像或缺失表格数据）的鲁棒性。在阿尔茨海默病的两个挑战性纵向临床任务（疾病阶段转换预测和淀粉样蛋白状态预测）上评估了所学表示的临床实用性，结果表明该方法优于强基线的多模态和单模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策依赖于对患者健康状况的多模态医学数据建模以预测疾病进展轨迹。然而，现有AI模型在开发过程中受限于医疗数据中频繁出现的模态缺失和表格数据不完整问题，且疾病标签提供的监督信号有限，难以有效学习高维多模态数据的表示。因此，亟需一种能处理不完整多模态数据并提升表示学习能力的新方法。

Method: MMAP采用图像编码器结合高效Sigmoid对比学习与生成重建进行预训练；表格编码器基于表格基础模型构建；引入缺失标记生成器，使模型可接受含缺失模态的数据输入，实现对缺失情况的鲁棒性。通过多模态对齐预训练，学习具有临床意义的联合表示。

Result: 在阿尔茨海默病的两个关键纵向任务——疾病阶段转换预测和淀粉样蛋白状态预测中，MMAP显著优于多种强基线模型（包括多模态和单模态方法），证明其在不完整数据下的表示学习能力与临床实用性。

Conclusion: MMAP是一种有效的多模态缺失感知预训练框架，能够有效应对医疗数据中的模态缺失与数据不全问题，在复杂临床任务中展现出优越的预测性能，为智能辅助临床决策提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Clinical decision making heavily relies on predicting the disease progression trajectory by seeking to understand patient's health status which is characterised by multimodal medical data. AI holds great potential for learning useful representations from multimodal medical data to predict disease progression and aid clinical decision making. However, development of predictive AI models is constrained by missing modalities and incomplete tabular data frequently occurring in medical datasets. In addition, disease labels alone may only provide limited supervisory signals for learning representations from high-dimensional multimodal data. Here, we present MMAP, a novel Multimodal Missing-aware Alignment Pretraining method for learning image-tabular representations from incomplete data. An image encoder is pretrained with efficient sigmoid contrastive learning combined with generative reconstruction. A tabular encoder is built upon a tabular foundation model. A missing token generator enables the two encoders to take incomplete data as input, enabling the model to be robust against missing modalities, either with missing images or missing tabular data. We evaluate the clinical usefulness of the learnt multimodal representations on two challenging longitudinal clinical tasks for Alzheimer's disease: predicting disease stage conversion and predicting amyloid status. The proposed method outperforms strong multimodal and unimodal baselines.

</details>


### [98] [GeoComposer: Geometry-Grounded Photographic Composition Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26620)
*Shuangzhi Li,Qiaoqiao Jia,Xingxin Chen,Guile Wu,Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoComposer，一种基于几何的摄影构图框架，通过几何感知表示学习和强化学习策略，实现视觉上吸引人且几何一致的构图优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在摄影构图中忽视了3D场景几何一致性，仅关注指令遵循和美学质量，限制了构图的自然性和真实性。

Method: 引入几何感知表示学习机制，利用视觉几何基础模型的几何先验来塑造中间表示；结合混合奖励的强化学习策略，联合优化指令遵循、美学质量和几何一致性。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在生成视觉吸引力强且几何一致的构图方面优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GeoComposer有效提升了摄影构图的质量，兼顾美学与几何一致性，为图像编辑提供了更自然、真实的解决方案。

Abstract: Photographic composition aims to provide visual guidance for improving the framing, viewpoint, and spatial arrangement of an image. Early methods primarily rely on image cropping to enhance composition, which is restricted to the viewpoint and spatial arrangement of the input image. Recent methods have explored image understanding and editing to improve composition, but they mainly focus on instruction following and aesthetic quality, overlooking the importance of 3D scene geometry consistency for photographic composition. In this work, we propose GeoComposer, a novel geometry-grounded photographic composition framework that analyzes the composition of a given image to generate textual guidance and synthesizes a visual exemplar that enhances the composition of the given image. To promote geometry-grounded composition, we propose a geometry-aware representation learning mechanism that leverages geometric priors from a visual geometry foundation model to shape the intermediate representations of the composition editing model. This mechanism preserves both global structural relationships and local fine-grained correspondences for geometry-grounded composition. Furthermore, we propose a reinforcement learning strategy guided by a hybrid reward that jointly optimizes instruction following, aesthetic quality, and geometric consistency. This enables the model to generate visual exemplars that faithfully follow the composition instructions while remaining visually appealing and geometrically consistent. Extensive experiments show the superiority of our approach over state-of-the-art methods, highlighting its effectiveness in generating visually appealing and geometrically consistent composition.

</details>


### [99] [A Data-Interventional Framework for Auditing Privacy and Fairness in Generative Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26623)
*Mischa Dombrowski,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据干预框架，系统分析扩散模型在医学影像合成中的隐私与公平性问题。通过引入合成解剖指纹（SAFs）作为可控探针，发现模型要么遗忘敏感特征，要么完全记忆包含这些特征的图像，而不会在新图像中泛化。进一步提出指标t'，基于扩散过程内部结构评估模型对记忆化的敏感性。结果显示，高意外性条件信号会放大记忆行为，低意外性信号则系统性抑制罕见特征，即使其在训练中反复出现。研究揭示了隐私与公平之间的权衡，并提供可操作的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成医学影像合成数据时面临隐私与公平性的根本矛盾：可能记忆罕见样本导致隐私泄露，或无法再现少数群体特征造成不公平分布。现有研究多孤立关注其中一方面，缺乏对二者交互机制的理解。

Method: 设计数据干预框架，使用合成解剖指纹（SAFs）作为可控探针，测试模型对敏感属性的泛化、记忆或压制行为；引入指标t'，通过分析不同意外性水平的条件信号，评估模型记忆倾向。

Result: 模型在面对罕见特征时表现出非此即彼的行为：要么完全遗忘，要么整体记忆原图，不实现跨图像泛化；高意外性条件信号显著增强记忆，低意外性信号则系统性抑制罕见特征。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了扩散模型在隐私与公平间的内在权衡，提出可量化的审计工具和缓解策略，为安全、公平的医学数据共享提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Diffusion-based synthetic data generation offers a promising route for sharing medical imaging data without releasing sensitive patient records. However, generative models face a fundamental tension between privacy and fairness: they may memorize rare training samples, leading to privacy risks, or fail to reproduce underrepresented features, resulting in unfair synthetic distributions. While prior work has largely focused on either memorization or fairness in isolation, their interaction remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we introduce a data-interventional framework to systematically analyze privacy and fairness in diffusion models. We discuss synthetic anatomical fingerprints (SAFs), rare and manually injected image features, as controlled probes to study whether models generalize sensitive attributes across identities, memorize training samples, or suppress rare signals entirely. Across multiple conditioning modalities, we observe a consistent behavior: models either forget these fingerprints or memorize the entire image in which they appear, but do not generalize them to novel images. To support large-scale auditing where explicit sample extraction is infeasible, we further introduce the indicator metric t', which estimates a model's susceptibility to memorization by exploiting the internal structure of the diffusion process. By comparing conditioning signals of varying surprisal, we reveal a clear relationship between conditioning rarity and memorization behavior. Highly surprising conditioning signals act as retrieval keys that amplify memorization, whereas low-surprisal conditioning signals systematically suppress rare features, even when these appear repeatedly in the training data. Our findings provide actionable insights and concrete mitigation strategies for safe and fair synthetic medical data sharing. Code is available at https://github.com/MischaD/Privacy.

</details>


### [100] [Laryngeal Structure Segmentation in High-Speed Videoendoscopy Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26636)
*Sardar Nafis Bin Ali,Mohsen Zayernouri,Dimitar D. Deliyski,Maryam Naghibolhosseini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用U-Net模型对喉部高速视频内镜（HSV）数据中的多个喉部结构进行自动分割，涵盖持续元音发音和连贯言语两种语音条件下的正常与异常发声样本。通过图像预处理提升图像质量，并结合定量评估与定性视觉检查验证模型性能。结果显示，模型整体准确率超过95%，证明其在自动化喉部图像分析、量化喉部动态特征及临床异常行为检测方面具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法尚未应用于连贯言语条件下的喉部高速视频内镜数据，而此类数据对捕捉非平稳的喉部运动行为和识别嗓音障碍相关异常模式至关重要。由于纤维内镜成像存在组织运动剧烈和图像质量受限等问题，自动化分割面临挑战，亟需高效可靠的算法支持。

Method: 采用U-Net深度学习模型对喉部关键结构（包括会厌皱襞、杓状软骨、声带、会厌及声门区）进行分割；使用来自正常和异常发声者的持续元音及连贯言语的HSV数据进行训练；应用噪声去除与直方图均衡化等图像预处理技术提升输入图像质量以增强模型性能。

Result: 所开发的模型在测试集上表现出优异性能，总体准确率超过95%；通过定量指标与可视化检查均证实其高精度与可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了适用于连贯言语条件下喉部高速视频内镜图像的自动化分割框架，具备高准确性与鲁棒性，为未来实现喉部动态的定量分析及嗓音障碍的早期检测提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Laryngeal high-speed videoendoscopy (HSV) offers an effective means of observing the motion of different laryngeal structures along with vibratory behaviors of the vocal folds under various voicing conditions. Segmentation of laryngeal tissues enables analysis of different tissue structures and their dynamics, helping characterize the involvement of laryngeal muscles in voice production. Given the large number of HSV frames, automating this task is imperative. While deep learning-based methods have been implemented in previous studies to segment laryngeal structures, they have not been applied to HSV data during connected speech, which poses significant challenges due to excessive tissue movements and image quality limitations associated with fiberoptic image acquisition. The application of deep learning to connected speech data is critical for capturing nonstationary laryngeal behaviors and identifying anomalous patterns associated with voice disorders. The present study aims to address these gaps by training U-Net models to detect the aryepiglottic folds and arytenoid cartilages, vocal folds, epiglottis, and glottal area, using HSV data from both sustained vowel phonation and connected speech obtained from normophonic and disordered voices. Image pre-processing techniques, including noise removal and histogram equalization, were applied to improve the quality of the training HSV images and enhance network performance. Finally, to evaluate the accuracy and reliability of the networks, quantitative performance metrics were used alongside qualitative visual inspection of the test images. The high performance of the developed networks, with overall accuracies exceeding 95%, establishes their potential as reliable tools for automated laryngeal image analysis, quantitative characterization of laryngeal dynamics, and future detection of anomalous laryngeal behaviors in clinical settings.

</details>


### [101] [Longitudinal Retinal Vascular Remodeling in Myopic Children Treated with Orthokeratology or Defocus Lenses: A Two-Year Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26662)
*Zhihao Zhao,Yinzheng Zhao,Jie Zhang,Huiqin Jiang,Yanyu Shangguan,Yanfei Sun,Li Chen,Yanlong Bi,M. Ali Nasseri,Bing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究回顾性分析了43名近视儿童在使用角膜塑形镜（OK）或多焦点离焦镜（Defocus）治疗后24个月内视网膜血管的纵向变化及其与眼轴延长的关系。结果显示，OK组眼轴增长显著慢于Defocus组；OK组血管参数变化较轻微，而Defocus组血管结构改变更明显，且其血管参数（尤其是COP和TVC）与眼轴长度关联更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同近视矫正方式对儿童视网膜血管结构的影响及其与眼轴增长的关系，以评估干预措施对眼部生物力学和血管重塑的长期影响。

Method: 采用回顾性队列研究设计，对43名近视儿童在基线、12个月和24个月进行临床检查及眼底照相。通过自动化分割模型提取主血管角度（MA）、分支角（BA）、分叉边缘角（BEA）、交叉点（COP）和终末血管数量（TVC）等血管参数。使用重复测量方差分析评估时间变化，皮尔逊或斯皮尔曼相关分析探讨眼轴长度与血管参数之间的关联。

Result: OK组24个月内眼轴增长为0.214 mm，显著低于Defocus组的0.522 mm（p < 0.01）。OK组中MA和BA略有下降，动脉的BEA逐渐降低，而COP和TVC相对稳定；Defocus组则表现为MA和BA显著下降，BEA上升，且COP和TVC明显减少（p < 0.05）。相关性分析显示，在Defocus组中，眼轴长度与多个血管参数（特别是COP和TVC）在所有时间点均呈较强相关，而在OK组仅BA和BEA与眼轴长度相关。

Conclusion: 角膜塑形镜能有效减缓眼轴增长，并引起较轻的视网膜血管重塑，相较于多焦点离焦镜更为温和。两种干预方式在血管参数变化的时间模式上存在差异，且与眼轴生长的相关性也不同，提示其可能通过不同的生物学机制影响眼球发育。

Abstract: Purposes: To characterize longitudinal retinal vascular changes in myopic children treated with orthokeratology (OK) or multifocal defocus lenses (Defocus) and to examine their association with axial elongation. Methods: In this retrospective cohort study, 43 myopic children underwent comprehensive clinical examination and fundus photography at baseline, 12 months, and 24 months. Axial length (AL) and spherical equivalent refraction (SER) were recorded at baseline, 6, 12, and 24 months. An automated segmentation model extracted vascular parameters, main vessel angle (MA), branching angle (BA), bifurcation edge angle (BEA), crossover point (COP), and terminal vessel count (TVC). Repeated-measures ANOVA assessed temporal changes. Pearson or Spearman correlations evaluated associations between AL and vascular metrics. Results: Over 24 months, the OK group exhibited significantly slower axial elongation than the Defocus group (0.214 mm and 0.522 mm, p < 0.01). In the OK group, MA and BA decreased modestly, BEA in arteries declined gradually, but COP and TVC remained relatively stable. The Defocus group demonstrated more pronounced decreases in MA and BA, an increase in BEA, and significant reductions in COP and TVC (p < 0.05). Correlation analysis revealed stronger associations between AL and vascular parameters, especially COP and TVC, in the Defocus group at all time points, whereas only BA and BEA correlated with AL in the OK group. Conclusions: OK lenses mitigate axial elongation and induce milder retinal vascular remodeling compared to Defocus lenses. Distinct temporal patterns of vascular metrics changes were observed between the two interventions, and correlate differentially with axial growth.

</details>


### [102] [DIFTA-3D: Depth-Consistent Instance-Level Feature Transfer and Adaptation of DINOv3 for 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26702)
*Linman Wang,ZiFei Zhang,Chunran Zheng,Xiwang Dong,Jiarong Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究将DINOv3视觉基础模型引入RGB-D 3D实例检测框架IIFNet3D，通过深度一致性特征管道实现点云与图像特征的融合。提出保守的VAID（支持加权语义蒸馏）策略仅作用于正样本RoI，提升语义匹配度。在ScanNetV2上，控制组mAP为76.15（IoU=0.25）和60.93（IoU=0.50），采用VAID后分别提升至76.59和62.16，性能增益分别为0.44和1.23点。结果表明该迁移策略有效，但未明确分离各组件贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如IIFNet3D依赖任务特定的Faster R-CNN/ResNet分支，导致与2D检测器绑定；虽可用冻结的视觉基础模型替代以减少依赖，但可能引入遮挡噪声及特征不匹配问题。因此需探索更鲁棒的跨模态特征融合机制。

Method: 设计深度一致性特征管道：将场景点投影至校准的RGB-D帧，进行度量深度残差检查，对通过验证的特征进行平均并缓存至离线点缓存；在提案对齐的RoI网格中聚合缓存特征。保留几何与双向实例融合路径，并引入保守的VAID作为低强度语义蒸馏策略，仅应用于正样本RoI。

Result: 在ScanNetV2数据集上，使用DINOv3的控制实验达到76.15（IoU=0.25）和60.93（IoU=0.50）的mAP；加入保守VAID后提升至76.59和62.16，分别提高0.44和1.23点。外部参考的IIFNet3D结果为75.7/63.8，因架构差异不直接可比。

Conclusion: 本工作验证了基于DINOv3的可控迁移策略在RGB-D 3D实例检测中的有效性，其深度一致性特征管道与保守语义蒸馏共同提升了检测性能，但未提供各模块独立贡献的因果分析。

Abstract: RGB-D 3D instance detectors benefit from visual semantics, but the task-specific Faster R-CNN/ResNet branch used by IIFNet3D couples feature extraction to a separately trained 2D detector and its image-domain labels. Replacing that branch with a frozen vision foundation model removes this task-specific dependency, but may introduce occlusion noise and a mismatch between patch features and geometry-aware detection features. In this work, we investigate this replacement through an adaptation of DINOv3 to the instance-level fusion pipeline of IIFNet3D. At the core of our approach is a depth-consistent feature pipeline that projects scene points into calibrated RGB-D frames, applies a metric depth-residual check, averages the accepted DINOv3 features into an offline point cache, and aggregates the cached features inside proposal-aligned RoI grids. The geometric and bidirectional instance-fusion paths are preserved, while Conservative VAID is evaluated as a low-strength, support-weighted semantic distillation recipe applied only to positive RoIs. We conduct extensive evaluations on ScanNetV2 to assess the proposed transfer recipes. On ScanNetV2, our DINOv3 control achieves mAP scores of 76.15 and 60.93 at IoU thresholds of 0.25 and 0.50, respectively. The Conservative VAID setting achieves mAP scores of 76.59 and 62.16, corresponding to numerical gains of 0.44 and 1.23 points over the control, respectively, in this checkpoint-level recipe comparison. The reported IIFNet3D result of 75.7/63.8 is used only as an external reference because the visual branch and processing protocol differ. Accordingly, we interpret these results as evidence for a controlled transfer recipe rather than as a causal estimate of the individual contributions of VAID or depth filtering.

</details>


### [103] [GAD-MambaUNet: Direction-Group Mamba with Gradient-Adaptive DINOv3 Distillation for Lightweight Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26729)
*Fang Wang,Huitao Li,Wenhan Chao,Zheng Zhuo,Xinxin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GAD-MambaUNet，一种轻量级医学图像分割网络，结合了高效局部建模、方向-分组状态空间交互和训练时基础模型监督。通过引入方向-分组图选择扫描（DG-GSS），将扫描方向与通道分组响应作为图节点，实现多方向融合前的结构化信息交换；同时采用DINOv3-GAD监督，利用冻结的DINOv3教师模型提供语义指导，并通过梯度自适应蒸馏动态调节蒸馏强度。该方法在准确率与效率之间取得良好平衡，实验验证了DG-GSS及训练时DINOv3-GAD监督的有效性。未来工作将探索更灵活的师生对齐策略，并扩展至多类别、多模态医学分割任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级医学图像分割网络在上下文建模能力上存在不足，且缺乏有效利用预训练视觉模型进行语义引导的机制。为提升小规模模型的性能，需要兼顾计算效率与语义理解能力。

Method: 提出GAD-MambaUNet，结合方向-分组图选择扫描（DG-GSS）以增强局部与方向敏感的信息交互；引入训练时基于DINOv3的渐进式知识蒸馏（DINOv3-GAD），通过梯度自适应调节蒸馏强度，实现更强的语义监督。

Result: GAD-MambaUNet在多个医学图像分割基准上达到优于或相当主流轻量级与通用分割方法的精度-效率平衡；消融实验证明DG-GSS与DINOv3-GAD监督均显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 所提GAD-MambaUNet通过结构化信息交互与动态知识蒸馏，在保持轻量化的同时显著提升医学图像分割性能，具有良好的应用前景，未来可拓展至更复杂的多类与多模态场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we proposed GAD-MambaUNet, a lightweight medical image segmentation network that combines efficient local modeling, direction--group state-space interaction, and training-time foundation-model supervision. To improve contextual modeling in compact segmentation networks, we introduced Direction-Group Graph Selective Scan (DG-GSS), which treated scan-direction and channel-group responses as graph nodes and enabled structured information exchange before multi-directional fusion. We further incorporated DINOv3-GAD supervision, where a frozen DINOv3 teacher provided semantic guidance during training, and Gradient-Adaptive Distillation dynamically regulated the distillation strength. GAD-MambaUNet achieves a favorable accuracy--efficiency balance compared with representative lightweight and general segmentation methods. Ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of DG-GSS and training-time DINOv3-GAD supervision. In future work, we will explore more flexible teacher--student alignment strategies and extend the proposed framework to more diverse medical segmentation scenarios, such as multi-class and multi-modal segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [104] [ASTRA-SR: Atmospheric Seeing and Turbulence Restoration for Astronomical Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26731)
*Xining Ge,Ziteng Cui,Shuhong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ASTRA-SR 是一种基于物理合成数据的盲单帧恢复框架，用于解决地面行星成像中的噪声、模糊和分辨率受限问题。通过高动态范围航天器原始图像作为真实数据，结合大气湍流、移动相位屏、时变点扩散函数和传感器噪声模拟低分辨率输入，模型先估计去噪但保留模糊的低分辨率图像，再通过多尺度处理和串行空间-振幅细化恢复高分辨率细节，相比最强基线提升0.49 dB前景PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面行星成像受大气湍流、传感器噪声和采样限制影响，导致图像退化，亟需一种联合去噪、去模糊和超分辨率的恢复方法。现有方法在真实场景下性能有限，因此需要基于物理原理构建合成数据并训练鲁棒模型。

Method: 使用高动态范围航天器RAW图像作为干净图像源，通过测量的大气湍流强度、移动相位屏、曝光平均的空间变化点扩散函数和传感器噪声合成低分辨率输入。ASTRA-SR模型分两步：首先估计噪声抑制但保留模糊的低分辨率图像；然后通过多尺度处理恢复空间结构，并采用串行空间-振幅细化重建高分辨率细节。

Result: ASTRA-SR在前景PSNR上相比最强基线提升0.49 dB，证明其在单帧恢复任务中具有优越性能。

Conclusion: ASTRA-SR是一种有效的盲单帧图像恢复方法，能够有效应对地面行星成像中的多重退化因素，其基于物理合成数据的训练策略显著提升了恢复质量。

Abstract: Ground-based planetary imaging suffers from atmospheric turbulence, sensor noise, and limited sampling, making restoration a joint denoising, deblurring, and super-resolution problem. We present ASTRA-SR, a blind single-frame restoration framework trained on a physics-grounded synthetic dataset. High-dynamic-range spacecraft RAW observations serve as clean sources, and paired LR inputs are synthesized using measured layer-integrated turbulence strengths, propagated moving phase screens, exposure-averaged spatially varying PSFs, and sensor noise.ASTRA-SR first estimates a noise-suppressed but blur-retaining LR image, then restores spatial structure through multiscale processing and reconstructs HR detail with serial spatial-amplitude refinement. It yields a 0.49 dB foreground PSNR gain over the strongest baseline approaches.

</details>


### [105] [Evaluating the Semantic-to-Geometric Gap in Adversarial Defenses Against Vision-Language Model-Based Plagiarism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26733)
*Christopher Burger,Christina Trotter,Joseph Carlisle,Charles Walter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在学术诚信方面的挑战，提出通过启发式对抗性图像变换来削弱模型性能，同时保持人类可读性。通过两阶段评估，发现所有模型均对对抗扰动存在脆弱性，尽管部分模型表现较鲁棒。结论认为，视觉扰动可作为短期缓解措施，但长期需重新设计评估方式以应对不断进步的VLM能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉-语言模型能力迅速提升，学生可通过简单上传图形问题图像进行‘轻量抄袭’，威胁学术诚信。亟需为教育者提供可操作的数据，以揭示VLM的局限性并支持评估安全策略。

Method: 采用两阶段评估方法：第一阶段手动评估基础VLM在电路图上的表现；第二阶段自动化大规模评估逻辑门拓扑结构与卡诺图坐标几何问题。使用启发式对抗性图像变换破坏模型识别能力，同时确保人类仍可理解图像内容。

Result: 所有测试的VLM均表现出对对抗性扰动的脆弱性，尽管部分模型具备一定鲁棒性。视觉扰动能有效降低模型性能，且不影响人类解读。

Conclusion: 视觉扰动可作为应对VLM滥用的近中期可行方案，但长期来看，教育者必须重新设计评估体系，以适应持续增强的VLM性能。

Abstract: The rapidly advancing capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) present a systemic challenge to academic integrity. VLMs now allow students to bypass meaningful engagement by capturing and submitting graphical problems as singular images, a practice we define as trivial plagiarism. To provide educators with actionable data on VLM limitations, we investigate the efficacy of heuristic adversarial image transformations designed to degrade model performance while remaining human-interpretable. Through a two-phase evaluation of introductory assessments, we manually assess baseline VLM performance on circuit diagrams, followed by an automated large-scale evaluation of topological structures (logic gates) and coordinate geometry (Karnaugh maps). We find that while highly capable VLMs can exhibit appreciable robustness, all models suffer vulnerability to adversarial perturbations. We conclude that while visual perturbations act as a viable near-term stopgap, long-term assessment security requires educators to reapproach assessment design given continually increasing VLM performance.

</details>


### [106] [FleXray: Universal Clinical X-ray Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26756)
*Victor Ion Butoi,Vivek Gopalakrishnan,John V. Guttag,Adrian V. Dalca,Neel Dey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FleXray，一种用于临床X射线中全身解剖结构分割的通用模型。通过构建基于物理的生成式X射线数据引擎，利用现有3D全身体积CT分割数据和生成图像编辑模型，模拟出多样化的带标注2D X射线图像，从而避免了大规模人工标注的需要。FleXray在未见过的研究数据集和真实世界X射线中准确分割60个解剖结构，并支持定量分析，如疾病分级、引导介入手术导航及病理目标的数据高效学习。相关模型、代码、数据集和浏览器工具已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线虽是医学影像中最广泛使用的模态，但其定量性差，因3D解剖结构投影至2D导致重叠与边界模糊，使得手动标注数据库困难，限制了形态与功能分析的应用范围。

Method: 构建基于物理的生成式X射线数据引擎，结合3D全身体积CT分割数据与生成图像编辑模型，模拟多样化带标注2D X射线图像；使用这些合成数据训练FleXray模型进行全身解剖结构分割。

Result: FleXray可在未见数据集和真实世界X射线上准确分割60个解剖结构，并实现对X射线的直接定量分析，支持疾病分级、介入导航和高效病理学习。

Conclusion: FleXray突破了传统X射线分析受限于局部区域与低量化能力的瓶颈，通过生成式数据驱动方法实现了全身解剖分割的通用化，为临床应用提供了强大工具。

Abstract: X-ray is medicine's most widely used imaging modality, yet remains among its least quantitative. Unlike volumetric modalities like CT or MRI, X-ray collapses 3D anatomy into a 2D projection, causing structures to overlap and anatomical boundaries to be ambiguous, even to experts. As a result, labeling X-ray databases for training general-purpose segmentation systems is impractical, leaving morphometric and functional X-ray analysis confined to narrow anatomical regions and applications. To this end, we present FleXray, a generalist model for anatomical segmentation across the entire body in clinical X-rays. Instead of curating large, manually annotated X-ray datasets, we build a scalable, physics-based generative X-ray data engine. Using existing 3D whole-body CT segmentation datasets and generative image-editing models, we simulate fully-annotated 2D X-rays with diverse appearances, physiological properties, and imaging geometries. Trained on these simulations, FleXray accurately segments 60 anatomical structures across unseen research datasets and in-the-wild X-rays. We further show that FleXray makes X-rays directly amenable to quantitative analysis, enabling automated measurements for disease grading, robust navigation during X-ray-guided interventions, and data-efficient learning of pathological targets. We release the model, code, a full-body X-ray segmentation dataset, and a local, easy-to-use browser-based tool at https://flexray.csail.mit.edu .

</details>


### [107] [StableVQ: Practical Guidelines for Stable Vector-Quantized Tokenizer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26774)
*Bao Tang,Jiahao Guo,Haoxiang Cao,Wenyu Liu,Changqian Yu,Kun Gai,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StableVQ提出了一种改进的向量量化方法，通过动态STE、区域VQ损失和解耦调度机制，解决了编码器-解码器与码本训练之间的耦合问题，提升了训练稳定性、码本利用率和重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共享投影码本方法在训练稳定性方面仍存在显著挑战，根源在于编码器-解码器与码本之间的学习目标纠缠，导致系统依赖于两模块的临时协作，难以在高压训练条件下维持稳定。

Method: 提出动态STE以修正编码器学习目标，使编码器在低码本利用率下仍能稳健优化重构空间；引入区域VQ损失重新定义码本学习目标，使其独立追踪编码输出分布；采用解耦调度策略为不同模块分配独立的学习率，确保系统整体鲁棒性。

Result: 在ImageNet上的实验表明，StableVQ在多种码本大小和初始化设置下均实现了训练稳定性、码本利用率和重建质量的持续提升，且不引入额外可学习参数。

Conclusion: StableVQ通过重新设计各模块的学习目标与优化策略，有效解耦了编码器-解码器与码本之间的依赖关系，显著提升了向量量化系统的可靠性与性能。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) is fundamental to discrete visual tokenizers that power modern autoregressive and masked image generation models. While recent shared-projection codebook methods have substantially advanced codebook utilization, training stability remains a critical and underexplored challenge. We argue that the root cause lies in the entanglement of the Encoder--Decoder and Codebook training: because neither module can reliably fulfill its own responsibility in isolation, the system can only function when the two subsystems happen to cooperate---a fragile condition that breaks down precisely when training is most stressed. We propose StableVQ, which revisits the proper learning objective of each module and resolves the problems that arise when each is trained to fulfill its own role independently. Concretely, (1) Dynamic STE corrects the instability in the Encoder's learning objective, enabling it to robustly optimize the reconstruction space under discrete regularization even when codebook utilization is low. (2) Region VQ Loss reconceives the Codebook's learning objective so that it can independently guarantee full tracking of the encoder output distribution, without relying on encoder oscillations to drive activation. (3) Decoupled Schedule recognizes that the distinct responsibilities of the Encoder--Decoder and the Codebook demand distinct optimization dynamics, and assigns each an independent learning rate schedule to ensure robust system-level behavior. Built on top of shared-projection codebooks, StableVQ is lightweight and introduces no learnable parameters. Experiments on ImageNet demonstrate consistent improvements in training stability, codebook utilization, and reconstruction quality across diverse codebook sizes and initialization settings.

</details>


### [108] [HARMONY: Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for MONocular Image-to-Scene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26793)
*Shufan Sun,Chen Wang,Enxin Song,Jiatao Gu,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HARMONY提出了一种分层思维链框架，结合代理推理与视觉几何基础模型，从单张图像中实现高保真、语义一致的3D场景重建。通过相机校准建立空间参考，利用VLM进行布局推理与物体分层放置，并引入深度优先遍历和反馈机制减少误差累积。每一步放置后通过点云估计进行几何优化，使渲染图像更贴近输入。实验表明其优于现有基线方法，在真实与合成图像上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前3D场景重建面临两大挑战：代理推理虽能理解空间关系但缺乏图像对齐；视觉几何模型虽可生成密集点云但重建质量有限。如何在单张图像下实现精确的空间关系与高质量重建仍是难题。

Method: HARMONY采用分层思维链框架：先校准相机建立语义空间坐标系；再通过代理式视觉语言模型（VLM）推理房间布局与初始摆放顺序；按墙挂物、独立家具、依附装饰分层放置，使用深度优先遍历确保依赖结构先于后续对象；每步放置后通过点云估计进行几何精修，形成反馈循环以抑制误差传播。

Result: HARMONY在合成与真实世界图像上均表现出色，生成的3D场景兼具语义一致性与视觉感知对齐性。定量与定性结果均优于现有基线方法，且相比GPT-6 Astra在物体排列准确性和细节保留方面更具优势。

Conclusion: HARMONY成功将语义理解与几何精度融合，实现了复杂室内场景的高质量单图像3D重建，推动了组合式3D场景重建的发展。

Abstract: Compositional 3D scene reconstruction has recently been explored from two directions: agentic reasoning that provides semantic understanding of spatial relationships but lacks precise alignment with input images; and visual geometry foundation models that predict dense point maps from input images but the reconstruction quality is limited. Therefore, recovering a complete 3D scene from a single monocular image with accurate inter-object relationships and high-fidelity reconstruction quality remains challenging. In this paper, we present HARMONY, a hierarchical chain-of-thought framework that leverages both agentic reasoning and visual geometry foundation. Given an image of an indoor scene, starting from an empty 3D floorplan, HARMONY first calibrates the camera against the reference image to establish a semantically-grounded spatial frame, then uses agentic VLM reasoning to recover the 3D room layout and an initial placement order. It then places the objects in a hierarchical order, from wall-mounted elements, free-standing furniture, to dependent decorations on top of furniture. We also use depth-first traversal for furniture so each placement conditions on previously resolved structure and a reflective feedback loop to avoid error accumulation. After each object placement by VLM, we use the point cloud estimations to perform geometry-based refinement so that the rendered image aligns better with the input. HARMONY can produce 3D scenes that are semantically consistent and perceptually aligned with the reference image, extending single-image compositional reconstruction to complex indoor scene images. Experiments on synthetic and real-world images demonstrate that HARMONY outperforms the evaluated reconstruction baselines, while qualitative comparisons with GPT-6 Astra suggest more faithful object arrangements and better preservation of scene details.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [What Does 99% Accuracy Measure? A Reproducible Audit of Shortcut Learning in a Widely Used Fake News Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25006)
*Yuvraj Verma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过TF-IDF与线性分类器管道审计了ISOT/Kaggle“假新闻与真新闻”数据集的三个泄漏渠道和两种分布偏移协议，发现模型高准确率主要源于主题元数据、新闻源标签和重复文档等泄露信号，而非真正判断新闻真伪的能力。即使去除这些泄漏因素，仍存在由编辑风格引起的残余信号，但该信号在跨主题迁移时显著失效，表明现有模型依赖的是数据集内的特定模式而非真实语义。基于DistilBERT的模型在原数据集表现更好，但在跨主题迁移中性能大幅下降，且在独立的LIAR基准上接近随机水平。结论是当前指标反映的是来源和主题可区分性，而非事实验证能力，并建议未来研究采用元数据仅限、小样本及主题不重叠基线作为低成本诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本分类模型在‘假新闻与真新闻’数据集上报告的高准确率（F1 > 0.98）与其实际判断新闻真伪的难度不符，暗示可能存在数据泄露或模型捷径学习现象，亟需系统性评估其可靠性。

Method: 采用透明的TF-IDF与线性分类器流程，针对数据集中的元数据、新闻源标签、重复文档三类泄漏通道进行消融实验；同时设计主题不重叠与时间转移两种分布偏移协议，测试模型泛化能力；并对比了线性模型与微调的DistilBERT模型的表现。

Result: 去除泄漏后F1仅下降1.21点，残余信号来自编辑风格而非关键词；跨主题迁移中线性模型损失5.2点精度，而DistilBERT损失达12.9点；所有模型在独立的LIAR基准上表现接近随机（ROC-AUC 0.54–0.57），无法超越多数类基线。

Conclusion: 当前数据集上的高得分主要反映的是来源和主题的可区分性，而非对新闻真实性的有效判断；模型容量提升反而加剧对捷径的利用；建议未来研究使用元数据仅限、小样本和主题不重叠基线作为简易诊断手段。

Abstract: Text classifiers trained on the ISOT/Kaggle "Fake and Real News" corpus routinely report accuracy and F1 above 0.98, a level of performance that sits uneasily beside the difficulty of assessing veracity. Using a transparent TF-IDF and linear-classifier pipeline as a measurement instrument, we audit the corpus along three leakage channels and two distribution-shift protocols, releasing all code and derived numbers. First, the benchmark is partly degenerate: a classifier given only the subject metadata field, with the article text discarded, attains F1 = 1.000, since the two classes have disjoint subjects. Second, removing all three leakage channels, metadata, a newswire source tag present in 99.2% of real articles, and 6,251 duplicate documents contaminating 19.4% of a naive test split, lowers F1 by only 1.21 points (0.9935 to 0.9814); the residual signal is diffuse editorial style rather than a few giveaway tokens, since deleting the 1,000 highest-weight unigrams still leaves F1 = 0.926. Third, this style signal does not transfer: under a topic-disjoint protocol, average precision falls from 0.9995 to 0.9475 and deployed F1 from 0.9905 to 0.8067, with a prior-matched analysis confirming a genuine 5.2-point loss of discrimination, while temporal transfer is nearly lossless. A fine-tuned DistilBERT is stronger in-distribution (F1 = 0.9993) but degrades far more under topic shift, losing 12.9 average-precision points against the linear model's 5.2. Transferred to the independent LIAR benchmark, all three models fall to near-chance ranking (ROC-AUC 0.54-0.57), none beating a majority-class baseline. We conclude that within-corpus scores here quantify source and topic separability rather than veracity, that added capacity exploits the shortcut rather than avoiding it, and we recommend metadata-only, small-sample, and topic-disjoint baselines as inexpensive diagnostics for future work.

</details>


### [110] [Training a Language Model End-to-End in Rust: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25008)
*Arif Adito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了一次在纯Rust环境下从头开始训练语言模型的尝试，耗资164美元租用GPU时间。尽管成功训练出一个约0.4B参数、以孟加拉语为主的语言模型（在孟加拉语上表现良好，但英语任务表现随机），但重点在于揭示了当前两大Rust机器学习框架Candle和Burn在训练阶段存在的严重缺陷：如梯度缺失、性能低下和崩溃问题，且这些错误均未被常规损失曲线检测发现。作者提出一种名为“梯度流仲裁器”的验证机制，通过检查每个可训练参数是否获得有限非零梯度来捕捉此类隐性故障。此外，还发现了孟加拉语分词中的‘分词肥力陷阱’，即原始字节级分词导致信息密度极低，修复后显著改善。结论是：目前Rust尚不适合作为训练语言模型的主流平台，但在推理部署方面具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在无Python、无PyTorch支持下，使用纯Rust实现端到端语言模型预训练的可行性；同时评估当前主流Rust ML框架在训练场景下的可靠性与成熟度。

Method: 采用纯Rust构建训练流程，使用Candle和Burn作为训练后端，在租用的H100 GPU上进行训练；引入梯度流仲裁器进行故障检测；对孟加拉语数据集进行分词优化，并对比不同配置下的模型表现。

Result: 成功训练出一个0.4B参数的孟加拉语优先语言模型，其在孟加拉语上的负对数似然为0.93，远优于随机初始化模型（12.60）；但英语常识多选题得分接近随机水平，符合预期。发现多个框架级缺陷，包括无声梯度丢失、极低的反向传播效率、以及大规模训练时的段错误。分词优化后，孟加拉语每令牌字符数从1.4提升至4.1。

Conclusion: 目前Rust尚未成为训练语言模型的可行替代方案，尤其在训练复杂度和稳定性方面仍存在重大缺陷。然而，其在模型推理部署方面展现出良好潜力。未来应聚焦于提升框架的训练健壮性与验证工具链，而非盲目追求纯Rust训练。

Abstract: I pretrained a language model end-to-end in Rust - alone, with no team, no PyTorch, and no Python in the training path - for $164 in rented GPU time. I report that as an achievement, not a recommendation: the more useful contribution is a measured failure taxonomy of the two leading Rust ML frameworks, Candle and Burn, as training (not inference) backends in 2026. I document five Candle defects, including fused kernels that silently produce no gradient, and three Burn defects, including a backward pass at roughly 3% of theoretical GPU throughput and a kernel-fusion path that segfaults mid-training at multi-billion-parameter scale. Every one passed ordinary loss-curve inspection; none announced itself. I describe the verification discipline that caught six such silent failures, centered on a gradient-flow arbiter: a test that runs one forward/backward pass and asserts every trainable parameter receives a finite, nonzero gradient, generalizable to any framework. The trained model (roughly 0.4B parameters, Bangla-first) shows strong Bangla language-modeling signal - a per-token negative log-likelihood of 0.93 against 12.60 for a random-initialized twin - while scoring at chance on English commonsense multiple-choice, the expected outcome of a deliberately small, Bangla-weighted budget (about 2 billion tokens, 54.6 hours, one rented H100). I also report a tokenizer-fertility trap in Bengali script: naive byte-level tokenization collapsed Bangla to roughly 1.4 characters per token against English's 3.9, silently inverting the corpus's language balance; fixing it reached roughly 4.1. To my knowledge, this is among the first documented end-to-end LM pretraining runs in pure Rust. After this run I moved training to PyTorch and kept Rust for on-device serving: in my hands, Rust is not yet a competitive place to train a language model, though it may be a good place to serve one.

</details>


### [111] [Same Quantity, Different Answer: Numerical Representation Invariance in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25009)
*Ephraim Atta-Duncan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究生成了3,600个精确有理数问题和8,600个跨五种保恒变换族的提示，评估了五个开源大模型。在固定语法审计后，规范答案准确率高达0.969-0.996，但轨道正确率降至0.848-0.981，轨道不变性为0.851-0.981；错误的不变轨道占比极低（≤0.003）。主要问题源于科学记数法乘法形式超出模型数字语法范围，导致评估接口误判为推理失败。此外，存在显著语义缺陷：Mistral Small 4在单位转换输入下得分仅0.699，产生265个与标签相差十的幂次的错误。在另一次9,000次调用实验中，表示一致性未优于重述一致性，且产生更多误报。附带数据集包含冻结基准、评估记录、审计日志、原始响应、清单及一键构建脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证数值等价问题在不同表达形式（如小数、分数、百分比、科学记数法、单位转换）下是否能获得一致的正确答案，揭示大模型在数字表达处理中的潜在缺陷，特别是语法与语义层面的系统性偏差。

Method: 生成3,600个精确有理数问题和8,600个跨五类保恒变换的提示，评估五个开源大模型；通过固定语法审计标准化答案格式，计算规范准确率、轨道正确率与轨道不变性；分析错误模式，识别语法边界与语义病理；进行对比实验验证表示一致性与重述一致性的表现。

Result: 规范答案准确率高（0.969–0.996），但轨道正确率与不变性显著下降（0.848–0.981），主要因科学记数法乘法形式超出模型语法支持；Mistral Small 4在单位转换任务中表现差，出现大量十的幂次错误；表示一致性在低误差子集上不优于重述一致性，且误报更高。

Conclusion: 大模型在数值等价问题上的表现受其内部数字语法限制影响显著，评估接口可能将语法边界问题误判为推理失败；模型仍存在深层语义偏差，尤其在单位转换和科学记数法处理方面；当前一致性评估策略需谨慎设计，避免引入误导性结论。

Abstract: Numerically equivalent word problems should yield the same canonical answer whether a quantity is written as a decimal, fraction, percentage, number word, scientific notation, or an exactly converted unit. We generate 3,600 exact-rational problems and 8,600 prompts spanning five identity-preserving transformation families, and evaluate five open-weight systems. After a fixed syntax audit that normalizes common answer forms without an LLM judge, canonical accuracy is 0.969-0.996, but orbit correctness falls to 0.848-0.981 and orbit invariance to 0.851-0.981; invariant-but-wrong orbits account for at most 0.003. Most of the broad strict-parser collapse arises because multiplication-form scientific notation lies outside the implemented number grammar, illustrating how evaluator interfaces can masquerade as reasoning failures. A distinct semantic pathology remains: Mistral Small 4 scores 0.699 on unit-converted inputs and produces 265 errors differing from the label by exact powers of ten. In a separate 9,000-call experiment that allocates equal calls to the compared arms, representation consensus does not outperform paraphrase consensus on a low-error subset and produces substantially more false alarms. The accompanying ancillary archive contains the frozen benchmark, evaluation and audit records, consensus raw responses, manifests, analysis code, and a one-command paper build.

</details>


### [112] [A Computational Approach to Measuring Semantic Change in Sanskrit Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25012)
*Tanay Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究检验了动态词嵌入方法在梵语中的适用性，这是一种古代、低资源语言，面临音位融合（sandhi）、形态屈折、复合词和多义性等挑战。研究构建了一个包含270万词元的跨四个经典时期的语料库，使用神经字节级sandhi分词器和词干提取器恢复词边界，并训练各时期的词嵌入。通过历史学术文献构建验证集，以锚点位移法测试语义变化方向。21个可测试的语义变化中有19个与历史学记载方向一致（符号检验，p=0.00011），表明该方法在梵语中具有较高有效性。研究还揭示了语言特性对模型配置的影响，并提出改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态词嵌入方法主要在现代、高资源、良好分词的语言上验证，但其在古代、低资源且存在复杂语音和形态结构的语言（如梵语）中的适用性尚不明确。因此，需要评估该方法是否能有效捕捉梵语中的语义变迁。

Method: 构建一个270万词元的跨四个时期的梵语文本语料库；使用神经字节级sandhi分词器和词干提取器恢复词边界；针对每个时期训练词嵌入模型；基于历史学术文献构建验证集，采用锚点位移法评估语义变化方向的一致性。

Result: 在21个可测试的语义变化中，有19个方向与历史学记载一致（符号检验，p=0.00011），证明动态词嵌入在梵语中具有良好的语义变化捕捉能力。同时，研究揭示了语言特性对模型配置的约束，并指出了未来改进的方向。

Conclusion: 动态词嵌入方法可以有效应用于梵语，尽管面临音位融合、形态复杂性等挑战，但仍能准确捕捉历史语义变化。研究结果为古语言的语义演化分析提供了新工具，并提示需根据语言特点优化模型配置。

Abstract: Diachronic word embeddings have become the modern standard for tracking semantic change, yet they have been largely validated on modern, high-resource, and well-segmented languages. This paper tests whether the paradigm transfers to Sanskrit, an ancient, low-resource language whose phonological fusion (sandhi), morphological inflection, compounding, and polysemy pose a unique challenge. I assemble a 2.7M-token corpus spanning four canonical periods, recover word boundaries with a neural byte-level sandhi splitter and lemmatizer, and train per-period embeddings across configurations. To evaluate the system, I curate a validation set from historical scholarship and test recovery directionally with anchor displacement. Of 21 testable shifts, 19 move in the philologically attested direction (sign test, p=0.00011). I further show which configuration the language forces and comment on opportunities for improvement.

</details>


### [113] [Retrieved-Span Training for Efficient Query-Focused Meeting Summarization on QMSum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25028)
*Edward Xi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对QMSum数据集缺乏评估指标的问题，重新评分或生成了15个系统，并在统一推理框架下对比不同模型表现。结果显示，406M的Fusion-in-Decoder模型在从截断长输入切换到2000词检索段落后ROUGE-1下降6.30，但通过在该段落模式下微调可恢复性能；其在测试集上得分为36.33 ROUGE-1，优于1.2B模型的35.41，但两者差异不显著（95%置信区间[-0.27, +2.22]）。小模型参数量仅为大模型的三分之一，峰值推理内存也少于一半。进一步微调和替换输入内容分别带来显著性能提升。此外，一个406M模型在特定提示和参考重叠评分下超越五个专有模型至少6.2 ROUGE-1，但受限于输出长度及缺乏人工或事实性评估，结论有限。最终结论仅适用于QMSum数据集和自动指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QMSum数据集缺乏统一的评估标准，导致查询聚焦型会议摘要结果难以比较，亟需建立一致的评估框架以公平比较不同系统性能。

Method: 在统一推理接口下对15个系统进行重评分与生成；对比不同规模模型（如406M与1.2B）在不同输入策略（如截断长输入与2000词检索段落）下的表现；通过微调适应新输入范式，并使用自动指标（如ROUGE-1）进行量化分析。

Result: 406M模型在2000词检索段落输入下经微调后性能恢复，测试得分36.33 ROUGE-1，优于1.2B模型的35.41，但差异不显著；小模型参数更少、内存占用更低；微调和输入替换分别带来5.29和1.55的性能增益；单个406M模型在特定设定下超越多个专有模型，但受限于评估维度。

Conclusion: 当前结论仅基于QMSum数据集和自动指标，缺乏人工与事实性评估，因此无法全面判断模型优劣，且结果受评估方式影响较大。

Abstract: QMSum provides no scorer, making query-focused meeting summarization results difficult to compare. We rescore or generate 15 systems under one implementation. Through a common inference port, a released 406M Fusion-in-Decoder specialist loses 6.30 ROUGE-1 when moved from capped long input to 2,000-word retrieved spans. Fine-tuning it on this span regime recovers the loss. On test it scores 36.33 ROUGE-1 versus 35.41 for our 1.2B system; the meeting-cluster 95% interval for the difference is [-0.27, +2.22], so QMSum does not statistically separate them. The smaller system uses about one-third as many total parameters and less than half the peak inference memory. Within the fixed 1.2B base, span-regime fine-tuning adds 5.29 [+4.02, +6.56], while replacing the first 4,500 transcript words with 2,000 retrieved words adds 1.55 on test and 0.29 on validation. Separately, under one concise prompt and reference-overlap scorer, a released 406M specialist exceeds five proprietary hosted models by at least 6.2 ROUGE-1, but output length and absent human or factuality evaluation limit this ordering. Conclusions are limited to QMSum and automatic metrics.

</details>


### [114] [From Tone to Trajectory: Continuous Sentiment and the Shape of Monetary Policy Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25034)
*Martin Feldkircher,Márton Kardos,Kristoffer Laigaard Nielbo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究央行新闻发布会中情感走势的形状（而非仅平均情绪）是否蕴含政策信号。通过构建欧央行与美联储发布会的情感弧线（货币立场、经济前景、不确定性三个维度），发现情感走势的结构能有效预测利率决策、通胀预期及预测者分歧，且其预测力超越传统词库基准。这表明政策信号不仅取决于言论整体倾向，更取决于情绪在文本中的序列与强调方式。此外，情感弧线还影响专业预测者的预期调整与分歧程度，体现接收端效应。因此，沟通设计（语言的顺序与重点）是政策信号的核心特征，而非次要修饰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注央行声明的平均情绪或关键词，但忽视了情感在文本中如何随时间演变。本文旨在探索情感弧线的形状是否携带额外的政策信息，从而揭示沟通设计在货币政策传达中的关键作用。

Method: 构建欧元区央行（ECB）和美联储（Fed）新闻发布会的情感弧线，基于三个维度：货币立场、经济前景、不确定性。使用自然语言处理技术提取逐段情感倾向，并分析其动态变化模式。通过回归模型检验这些弧线特征对后续利率变动、通胀预期及预测者分歧的预测能力。

Result: 情感弧线的形状显著预测了利率决策，优于传统的词典法基准；同时影响专业预测者的通胀预期调整幅度与分歧水平，表明存在接收端效应。该结果在两个机构中均稳健。

Conclusion: 沟通设计——即政策语言在文本中的顺序与强调方式——是政策信号的核心组成部分，而非附加细节。理解情感弧线的动态演变有助于更准确解读央行沟通的真实意图。

Abstract: Central bank press conferences are not merely information releases --- they are structured narratives. We study whether the shape of sentiment within a statement, not just its average tone, carries policy-relevant signals. Constructing sentiment arcs for ECB and Fed press conferences along three dimensions --- monetary stance, economic outlook, and uncertainty --- we assess their predictive content for policy rate changes, inflation expectations, and forecaster disagreement. Our findings show that arc shape robustly predicts rate decisions beyond lexicon-based benchmarks at both institutions --- it is not merely whether a statement sounds hawkish or economically optimistic on average, but how these sentiments are sequenced and emphasized across the statement, that carries the policy signal. Arc features also shape how professional forecasters update inflation expectations and how much they disagree, pointing to a receiver-side effect distinct from the direct policy signal. These findings suggest that communication design --- the sequencing and emphasis of policy language across a statement --- is a first-order feature of the policy signal, not a second-order refinement.

</details>


### [115] [Peerify: Benchmarking Peer-Review Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25046)
*Alireza Daghighfarsoodeh,Sajad Ebrahimi,Ali Ghorbanpour,Soroush Sadeghian,Radin Cheraghi,Negar Arabzadeh,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Peerify管道，用于基于手稿验证同行评审意见的准确性。通过分解评审意见为原子性声明，检索相关手稿证据，并判断每条声明是否被论文支持。构建了来自NeurIPS 2024和ICLR 2024的800条真实评审意见组成的基准数据集，其中300条经人工标注用于评估自动化标签质量。实验表明，以检索为中心的验证和声明分解至关重要，而模糊或解释性评论仍具挑战性。自动化标签与人类共识一致率达90.3%（κ=0.87），而现成的蕴含模型性能低于0.24宏平均F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前同行评审中，验证评审意见是否得到手稿证据支持主要依赖人工，耗时且效率低。需要一种自动化方法来提高评审可信度和效率。

Method: 提出Peerify管道，包括：将评审意见分解为原子性声明、利用检索技术获取相关手稿内容、使用自然语言推理判断声明是否被支持。构建了包含800条真实评审意见的基准数据集，其中300条经人工标注用于评估。

Result: 自动化标签与人类共识一致率达到90.3%（κ=0.87），说明方法有效；但现成的蕴含模型表现较差（宏平均F1<0.24），凸显检索与声明分解的重要性。

Conclusion: Peerify成功实现了对同行评审意见的手稿支撑验证，证明了检索驱动与声明分解在提升自动化验证效果中的关键作用，但仍需应对模糊和解释性意见的挑战。

Abstract: Peer review plays a central role in scholarly publishing, yet verifying whether reviewer claims are supported by manuscript evidence remains a largely manual and time-consuming process. We present Peerify, a pipeline for manuscript-grounded verification of peer-review claims. Given a manuscript and a review comment, the Peerify pipeline decomposes reviews into atomic claims, retrieves relevant manuscript evidence, and determines whether each claim is supported by the paper. To support the development and evaluation of the pipeline, we construct a benchmark of 800 claims derived from authentic peer-review interactions collected from NeurIPS 2024 and ICLR 2024, including a 300-claim hand-labeled subset used to audit the automated supervision. We evaluate state-of-the-art language models and retrieval strategies within the Peerify pipeline, together with entailment baselines. Our results demonstrate the importance of retrieval-centered verification and claim decomposition, while highlighting the challenges posed by ambiguous and interpretive reviewer claims. Automated labels agree with human consensus on 90.3% of audited claims ($κ= 0.87$), while off-the-shelf entailment models stay below 0.24 macro-F1.

</details>


### [116] [AIBuildAI-2.5: Efficient Autonomous AI Model Development Through LLM-Guided Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25047)
*Peijia Qin,Ruiyi Zhang,Qi Cao,Han Guo,Li Zhang,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AIBuildAI-2.5 is an advanced agentic system that improves efficiency in autonomous AI model building by addressing three key limitations: sparse execution feedback, lack of resource-aware scheduling, and high inference costs. It introduces a novel LLM-guided tree search using a judge to evaluate candidates based on expected improvement, grounding, and feasibility; a scheduler that optimizes training job launches based on hardware availability; and a router that assigns appropriate LLMs to tasks based on complexity. The system achieves a 73.3% medal rate on MLE-Bench and outperforms baselines on six AI research tasks from AIRS-Bench.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing autonomous AI agents face inefficiencies due to limited execution budget leading to noisy rewards, unoptimized hardware utilization, and high inference costs from relying on a single powerful LLM. These issues hinder scalability and practical deployment.

Method: AIBuildAI-2.5 employs a multi-component framework: a judge for scoring candidate programs based on expected improvement, grounding, and feasibility; a selector that ranks candidates using these scores and search state; a resource-aware scheduler for training jobs; and a router that dynamically assigns lower-cost LLMs to simpler tasks and high-capability models to complex ones.

Result: The system achieves a 73.3% medal rate on MLE-Bench and surpasses a strong baseline across six autonomous AI research tasks from AIRS-Bench, demonstrating significant improvements in both performance and efficiency.

Conclusion: AIBuildAI-2.5 effectively addresses critical efficiency challenges in autonomous AI model building through intelligent task routing, resource-aware scheduling, and LLM-guided search, setting a new benchmark for scalable and cost-effective AI agent systems.

Abstract: Autonomous agents that automatically build artificial intelligence (AI) models could broaden access to AI across science and engineering. A popular line of such agents frames model building as a code search problem and solves it by tree search, in which each node is a candidate program and the tree grows by generating a child program from a parent, and these agents now approach the capability of experienced AI engineers on realistic benchmarks. However, these agents have three weaknesses in efficiency that have not been fully addressed. First, only a small number of candidates can be executed within a realistic budget, so search rules that rank nodes by executed rewards, such as Monte Carlo-style tree search, rely on few and noisy scores and select the next node to explore less effectively. Second, no resource-aware strategy is used to schedule training jobs, which can lower hardware utilization and training efficiency. Third, every agent call is served by a single powerful model, which inflates inference cost. Here we introduce AIBuildAI-2.5, an agentic system that carries out the tree search with LLM agents and addresses each of the three issues. AIBuildAI-2.5 proposes a novel LLM-guided tree search, in which a judge scores each candidate on its expected improvement, grounding, and feasibility, and a selector ranks the pool of candidates from these scores and the state of the search. In addition, AIBuildAI-2.5 comprises a scheduler that launches training jobs with the current hardware resource status taken into account and a router that assigns lower-cost LLMs to less demanding tasks while reserving the most capable LLM for the most challenging sub-tasks in the AI model building workflow. AIBuildAI-2.5 ranks first on MLE-Bench with a medal rate of 73.3%, and outperforms a strong baseline on six autonomous AI research tasks from AIRS-Bench.

</details>


### [117] [Prompt Breadth and Rollout Refresh Interact in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25048)
*Lingxiang Hu,Tianle Xia,Ming Xu,Yiding Sun,Linfang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在策略蒸馏（OPD）中提示的数量及其对学生策略生成响应的影响，重点分析了提示广度和滚动更新机制的联合作用。通过一个3×3的数学推理实验，固定14,080条轨迹和110次优化更新，改变提示库和响应生成策略快照数量。结果显示，当使用10个快照时，8个提示即可达到24.09%的平均准确率，接近14,080个不同提示下的24.51%；若响应冻结于初始策略，增加提示广度会降低准确率（从21.16%降至19.05%）；而采用每步更新策略则提升准确率（从23.61%升至25.57%）。两者交互效应为4.07个百分点，置信区间[2.00, 6.28]。在两种教师模型下对比发现，周期性模型在短预算下表现更优，但冻结响应模型在32K输出限制下超越前者，且仅需1.7-1.8倍响应令牌数。结论表明，提示效率受刷新机制与推理预算共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在策略蒸馏（OPD）中提示数量与学生策略响应生成之间的关系，特别是提示广度与响应更新策略如何协同影响训练效率与性能。

Method: 设计了一个3×3的数学推理实验，固定14,080条轨迹和110次优化更新，系统地变化提示库大小与响应生成策略快照数量，比较不同条件下模型的平均准确率、答案完成度及响应令牌消耗。

Result: 使用10个快照时，8个提示即可达到24.09%的平均准确率，接近14,080个不同提示的24.51%；响应冻结时，提示广度增加导致准确率下降（21.16%→19.05%）；响应每步刷新时，准确率上升（23.61%→25.57%），交互效应达4.07个百分点（95%置信区间[2.00, 6.28]）。周期性模型在短预算下表现更优，但冻结响应模型在32K输出预算下超越，且用更少的响应令牌。

Conclusion: 提示效率在OPD中并非单一因素决定，而是受响应刷新机制与推理预算共同影响；合理协调提示广度与策略更新频率可显著提升训练效率与性能。

Abstract: How many prompts does on-policy distillation (OPD) need, and how does the answer depend on the student policies that generate its training responses? We study these two controls jointly: prompt breadth and rollout refresh. A 3x3 mathematical-reasoning experiment fixes 14,080 trajectories and 110 optimizer updates while varying the prompt bank and the number of response-generating policy snapshots. With ten snapshots, eight prompts reach 24.09% average accuracy, close to 24.51% for 14,080 distinct prompts. With responses frozen at the initial policy, however, increasing breadth lowers accuracy from 21.16% to 19.05%; under per-update refresh, it raises accuracy from 23.61% to 25.57%. The resulting interaction is 4.07 percentage points, with a 95% question-paired interval of [2.00, 6.28]. Matched comparisons under two teachers reveal a second reversal: the periodic models have higher short-budget accuracy and answer completion, but frozen-response models overtake in average accuracy at a 32K output limit, using 1.7-1.8x as many response tokens. These results show that prompt efficiency in OPD can depend on both refresh and inference budget.

</details>


### [118] [Mitigating LLM Over-Refusal via Dynamic Semantic Routing Calibratione](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25049)
*Zixuan Wang,Bingjie Zhang,He Zhao,Dandan Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型在安全对齐中出现过度拒绝现象的动态机制，发现部分敏感安全注意力头在处理高安全但无害提示时会错误激活，引发严重的注意力路由冲突，导致目标实体被错误绑定至拒绝语义。为此，提出无需训练的推理阶段校准框架SRC，通过动态抑制这些过度敏感的注意力头，并结合双分支逻辑融合作为安全正则化器，有效缓解过度拒绝问题，同时保持模型内在安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注静态表征重叠导致的大语言模型过度拒绝问题，忽视了其背后的动态机制。为深入理解并解决这一现象，亟需从注意力机制的角度揭示其内在机理，并设计针对性的干预方法。

Method: 采用机制分析视角，基于Transformer注意力结构识别出在处理特定安全相关提示时异常激活的‘超敏感安全头’；提出Semantic Routing Calibration（SRC）框架，在推理阶段动态定位并抑制这些头部；引入双分支逻辑融合策略，增强解码过程中的安全性约束。

Result: 实验表明，SRC能显著降低过度拒绝率，同时最大限度保留模型的内在安全性能，实现安全与可用性的平衡。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了大语言模型过度拒绝现象的动态注意力冲突本质，提出的SRC框架为解决该问题提供了高效、轻量且无需训练的解决方案，具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) aligned for safety often suffer from over-refusal, incorrectly rejecting benign yet safety-related instructions. Prior studies primarily attribute this to static representation overlap, largely overlooking the underlying dynamic mechanisms. In this paper, we present the mechanistic analysis of over-refusal through the lens of internal routing conflicts within transformer attention. We discover that a sparse subset of Hypersensitive Safety Heads misfires on Hard-Safe prompts, exhibiting abnormal attention entanglement that forcefully binds harmless target entities to refusal semantics. This triggers a severe, high-entropy routing conflict that deprives target entities of necessary attention. To counteract this, we propose Semantic Routing Calibration (SRC), a lightweight, training-free inference framework. SRC precisely localizes and dynamically suppresses these hypersensitive safety heads at the inference stage. Coupled with a dual-branch logits fusion that acts as a safety regularizer during subsequent decoding, SRC seamlessly restores trustworthy reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SRC alleviates over-refusal, with intrinsic safety performance preserved as much as feasible.

</details>


### [119] [FrontierMath Erdős](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25050)
*Tom Adamczewski,Thomas F. Bloom*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FME 是一个包含68个开放数学问题的基准测试，这些问题源自 erdosproblems.com，由第二作者筛选，具有较高的数学价值和难度。该基准要求AI在Lean证明助手环境中解决这些猜想（证明或反证）。评估了五种AI模型，每个问题预算300美元，仅有GPT-6 Astra取得3%的得分，其余均为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI在数学领域虽已解决部分开放问题，但缺乏系统性评估。FME旨在建立一个统一、可重复、公平的基准，用于衡量AI在解决高难度数学猜想方面的真实能力。

Method: 从652个开放问题中筛选出68个最具数学意义和挑战性的问题，构建标准基准；所有模型在相同环境（Lean）下、相同预算（$300/题）内进行自主求解，结果以是否成功证明或反证为判断标准。

Result: 五种被测AI中，仅GPT-6 Astra在68个问题中成功解决其中1个（3%），其余模型均未能解决任何问题。表明当前AI在处理高度复杂的数学猜想方面仍存在显著局限。

Conclusion: FME 提供了一个严谨、可复现的评估框架，揭示了当前AI在解决高阶数学开放问题上的薄弱表现，强调了未来在数学推理与自动证明方向上仍需重大突破。

Abstract: We introduce FrontierMath Erdős (FME), a benchmark of 68 Erdős problems that are open as of August 2026. To solve a task in FME, AI systems must resolve (prove or disprove) one of the 68 conjectures in the proof assistant Lean. Our 68 problems were selected by the second author among 652 open problems on erdosproblems.com for their mathematical interest and difficulty. AIs have recently resolved several open problems in mathematics, but these demonstrations fall short of a systematic study of AI capabilities. FME evaluates every AI model on the same fixed problems, autonomously and under the same budget. We evaluated five AIs with a budget of \$300 per problem. One (GPT-6 Astra) scored 3%, and all others scored 0%.

</details>


### [120] [LLM-Driven Training-free Location-Attribute Synergic Fusion: A Closed-Loop Paradigm for Dual-source Encrypted POIs and LULC Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25051)
*Chang Li,Xingtao Peng,Yongjun Zhang,Yinfei He,Cairun Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次提出一种基于大模型（LLM）驱动、无需训练的闭环优化范式，用于解决双源加密兴趣点（DSEP）融合中的位置与属性不确定性问题。该方法通过双向迭代反馈，联合优化位置变换与属性对应关系：先利用大模型进行属性匹配以降低复杂度至O(N)，再在ISODATA聚类的局部子区域中通过改进粒子群优化算法精化变换系数；随后基于更新的几何残差，采用大模型-模糊方法重新评估属性置信度，并将结果反馈至位置优化，形成闭环。通过样本净化与自适应半径收缩，仅需约两次迭代即可收敛。进一步提出一种无需训练的LULC制图方法，通过位置融合继承加密地图的土地利用类别，生成矢量-栅格一体化的LULC成果。在31个中国大陆省会及直辖市的参考无须评估中，本方法实现平均位置融合残差4.58米，属性融合准确率95.12%，较开源基线和先进方法分别提升1.77米和14.87%。整体上，为加密矢量数据的地理定位提供了无需实地控制点的免训练解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双源加密兴趣点（DSEP）存在位置与属性双重不确定性，包括非线性系统性偏移和命名不一致，严重制约土地利用/覆被（LULC）制图精度。现有方法难以同时处理位置与属性耦合问题，且多依赖训练或大量人工标注，缺乏高效、免训练的闭环优化机制。因此亟需一种能协同优化位置与属性、无需外部监督的融合框架。

Method: 提出一种基于大模型驱动的训练-free闭环优化范式：1）属性协同位置融合：利用大模型进行属性匹配，将匹配复杂度从O(N^2)降至O(N)，结合改进粒子群优化（PSO）在ISODATA聚类的局部子区域中优化位置变换参数；2）位置协同属性融合：基于更新后的几何残差，采用大模型-模糊方法重估属性置信度；3）双向反馈机制：将修正的对应关系反馈至位置优化，形成闭环迭代；4）样本净化与自适应半径收缩策略，加速收敛至约两轮迭代；5）构建无需训练的LULC制图流程，通过位置融合继承加密地图的土地利用类别，输出矢量-栅格集成结果。

Result: 在31个中国省级行政区的实验中，所提方法实现平均位置融合残差4.58米，属性融合准确率达95.12%，相较开源基线与当前最优方法分别降低1.77米误差并提升14.87%准确率。验证了方法在低误差、高精度下的有效性与鲁棒性。此外，该方法无需实地控制点即可完成加密矢量数据向WGS-84坐标系的地理定位，具备实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 本研究首次构建了面向双源加密兴趣点融合的训练-free、位置-属性协同优化闭环框架，有效解决了因非线性偏移与命名不一致带来的融合难题。通过大模型赋能的智能匹配与优化机制，实现了高精度、快速收敛的融合效果，并支持生成高质量的矢量-栅格一体化土地利用/覆被地图。该方法为加密地理数据的自动化地理配准与知识挖掘提供了新范式，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Dual-source encrypted points of interest (DSEP), POIs from two encrypted coordinate systems, suffer from intertwined location and attribute uncertainties, including nonlinear systematic misalignment and naming inconsistency, hindering land-use/land-cover (LULC) mapping. To the best of our knowledge, this paper is the first to propose an LLM-driven, training-free location-attribute synergic closed-loop optimization paradigm for DSEP fusion. The paradigm jointly refines location transformation and attribute correspondences through iterative feedback. Attribute-synergic location fusion uses an LLM-driven attribute matching method to establish DSEP correspondences, reducing matching complexity from O(N^2) to O(N), and refines transformation coefficients using an improved particle swarm optimization algorithm within ISODATA-clustered local subregions. Location-synergic attribute fusion then reassesses attribute confidence from updated geometric residuals through an LLM-fuzzy method. The refined correspondences feed back into location optimization, forming a bidirectional closed loop. Sample purification and adaptive radius contraction enable convergence in essentially two iterations. We further propose a training-free LULC mapping method that inherits land-use classes from encrypted maps through location fusion, producing vector-raster integrated LULC maps. A reference-free POI fusion evaluation method is applied across 31 provincial capitals and municipalities in mainland China. Experiments show that our method achieves an average DSEP location fusion residual of 4.58 m and attribute fusion accuracy of 95.12%, improving upon the open-source baseline and state-of-the-art method by 1.77 m and 14.87%, respectively. Overall, the method provides a training-free solution for DSEP fusion and enables georeferencing of encrypted vector data to WGS-84 without field-surveyed ground control points.

</details>


### [121] [Self-Cleaning and Captured Anyway: One Measured Primitive for Error in a Store an Agent Writes to Itself, and What a Falling Score Actually Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25052)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在无限任期条件下，代理将结论写入仅追加存储并随后检索时形成的闭环行为。由于写入不删除，状态空间存在硬上限 (n-1)/n，导致结果为两种极端状态之间的选择而非衰减。实验显示，在 f_0 = 0.9 时，真实事实运行中多数结果集中在狭窄区间，且多值运行的占用率显著高于单值运行。模型的核心机制依赖于一个无参数的复制函数 γ(φ)，其预测方向在绝大多数真实和合成数据上表现准确。尽管规模扩大无法提升存储捕获率（仍为 0.850），但干预措施中时间因素主导效果，一致性门控可使模型性能提升至 0.993。统计分析表明，种子作为重采样单位，设计效应为 3.75，部分结论在多种子下显著弱化，而另一些则保持稳定。所有 87 个评分条目均在附录 W 中，其中 37 条被标记为撤回、失败、自我修正或公认极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示在无限任期与仅追加存储背景下，代理闭环行为的长期演化特性，挑战传统的一向污染假说，并探索其在现实知识系统中的可区分性与稳定性。

Method: 通过构建无限任期下的仅追加存储模型，模拟代理写入与检索过程；使用真实（Wikidata）和合成数据验证复制函数 γ(φ) 的预测能力；采用多种子重采样与统计检验评估模型鲁棒性与结论可靠性；对结果进行分组分析，比较不同干预策略下的性能表现。

Result: 状态空间存在硬上限 (n-1)/n，导致系统在两极间选择而非衰减；在多种初始概率 f_0 下，区间占有量与多值运行相关性显著；复制函数 γ(φ) 在 353/360 真实运行中正确预测漂移方向；一致性门控可将模型性能提升至 0.993；多种子分析显示部分结论因设计效应而弱化，但关键结论仍具统计显著性。

Conclusion: 该闭环模型在无限任期下表现出非衰减的双态选择特征，其稳定性由一个无参数的复制函数驱动，且可通过一致性门控实现高性能。尽管规模扩展无效，但时间与一致性控制是关键变量。多种子分析揭示了统计结论的脆弱性，强调了对结果可重复性的严格评估。

Abstract: "An agent that writes its conclusions into a store it later retrieves from closes a loop usually reported as one-way contamination. Taking the loop to the infinite-tenure limit against an append-only store gives a different picture: because writing never deletes, the reachable state space has a hard upper edge at (n-1)/n, so the outcome is a choice between two edges rather than a decay. At f_0 = 0.9 the interval between the two modes holds 3.6% of 220 runs where a uniform spread would put 20.6%, and is strictly empty on the first 15; the pooled mean describes 8.2% of the runs it summarises, the median 68.2%. Everything the model contributes is carried by one measured primitive with no fitted parameter, the copy function γ(φ): on 36 Wikidata facts, sign(\hatγ - γ_{crit}), with γ_{crit} = 1/k at r = 0, w = 1, predicts the direction of drift on 353 of 360 real-fact runs (39 of 40 synthetic in the same batch). Scale does not rescue the store: pooled frontier capture is 0.850, with claude-sonnet-4.5 captured on 20 of 20 seeds against our registered prediction of <0.5. What the interval tests is distinguishability rather than count: on the real facts, multi-valued runs have 6.4x its occupancy of the rest. It survives at f_0 in {0.1, 0.3, 0.5}, capture peaks at f_0 = 0.5, and of four interventions with criteria frozen first, timing dominates fraction at matched budget while a consistency gate drives every model to 0.993. The resampling unit is the seed, at a design effect of 3.75 on a pooled level: under a 44-seed control the ordering supporting claim 4 collapses from Spearman +0.98 at three seeds to +0.31-0.80 at forty-four, while claim 2's ordering is exact there (+1.00, p = 0.017). All 87 graded rows are in Appendix W, 37 of them graded withdrawn, failed, self-correcting, undecidable or an acknowledged limit, against 50 that are not."

</details>


### [122] [LatentPort: Beyond KV Cache - Cross-Model Transfer of Recurrent Memory in Hybrid Language Models: A 4B-to-9B Hybrid-State Handoff Without Target Prefix Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25053)
*Simon P. Villani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次实现不同规模的混合语言模型（Qwen3.5 4B到9B）之间在不重读上下文的情况下，直接传递持续性递归推理状态。通过引入门控增量网络（GDN）和翻译注意力键值对，显著降低负对数似然（NLL），提升64个PG19文档的生成性能。结合直接递归与卷积状态复用，效果优于学习得到的GDN映射。新增434,176参数修正模块进一步提升表现，使9B模型在零历史前缀下超越4B模型的延续推理能力。实验限于单一方向、几何匹配的基础模型对及4K教师强制续写，泛化性与通用状态接口尚未验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可在不重读上下文的前提下，将一个语言模型的实时记忆无缝传递给另一个模型，以实现高效、连续的推理过程，尤其在异构但架构匹配的模型间建立持久状态共享机制。

Method: 采用架构匹配的Qwen3.5 4B与9B模型作为师生对，通过翻译注意力键值（KV）并结合门控增量网络（GDN）构建持久状态包；利用直接递归与卷积状态复用，设计因子选择机制融合状态，并引入小规模修正模块优化输出。所有操作均无需目标前缀重播。

Result: 在64个测试文档上，平均下一个词的对数损失（NLL）下降0.747纳特/词（95%置信区间[0.6921, 0.8047]），所有文档均获改善；修正后的9B模型在零历史输入下表现显著优于4B模型延续推理，且近似接近原生9B性能（额外NLL为0.076，JS散度0.022，上下文恢复率0.918）。

Conclusion: 本研究首次成功实现跨模型的无前缀重播持久状态转移，证明了在架构匹配条件下，部分状态坐标具备功能兼容性，且可通过轻量修正模块实现高性能继承。但该方法仍局限于特定方向、特定模型对，通用状态接口与自由生成等效性有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Can one language model hand its live memory to another without the receiver rereading the context? We demonstrate useful persistent hybrid-state transfer across one architecture-matched Qwen3.5 4B-to-9B sibling pair. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstrated cross-model handoff of persistent recurrent inference state between differently sized hybrid language models without target prefix replay. Translated attention KV alone leaves a large gap; adding the Gated DeltaNet (GDN) persistent-state package lowers teacher-forced negative log-likelihood (NLL), the average next-token log-loss, by 0.747 nats/token (95% paired document bootstrap CI [0.6921, 0.8047]), improving all 64 PG19 documents. Direct recurrent and convolution reuse outperforms the tested learned GDN maps, consistent with partial functional compatibility of persistent-state coordinates. A fresh component factorial selects translated KV with direct recurrent and convolution state. An additional 434,176-parameter correction improves that base on 64 fresh web documents: continuation loss is 0.076 nats/token above native 9B (excess NLL), Jensen-Shannon (JS) divergence is 0.022, and native context recovery (NCR) is 0.918. Corrected 9B significantly beats continued 4B inference while processing zero historical prefix tokens. Evidence covers one direction, one geometry-matched Base-model pair, and 4K teacher-forced continuation; the near-native gate failed, the 16K branch was not run, and free-generation equivalence and a general state interface remain unproven.

</details>


### [123] [MoM: Memory of Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25054)
*Bowen Qin,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Memory of Memory"（MoM）的新记忆机制，通过在写入时保留被覆盖值的溯源信息，解决了长时程大语言模型代理中记忆更新与状态保持的难题。其具体实现为Provenant Memory（P-Mem），一种基于类型化溯源图的记忆系统，能够同时追踪内容及其来源、状态和历史。该系统通过定义不同类型的操作（如支持、取代、反驳等）来管理多版本数据，确保当前状态的有效性而非单纯准确性。实验表明，P-Mem在读取时仅需约1/4的检索令牌即可达到最强检索记忆的准确率，且通过图引导的回合剪枝显著降低知识更新导致的过时回答率（从19.4%降至10.9%）。在修订链场景下，其正确率维持在100%，而传统查询时读取方法下降至25%；更重要的是，由于不覆盖旧值而是保留其溯源信息，它能恢复由传统写时更新（CRUD）记忆无法挽回的错误，表现出100%的纠错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程大语言模型代理的记忆系统存在两个核心问题：一是多数设计依赖查询时重构，导致过时信息反复出现并引发重复冲突；二是写时更新机制（如CRUD）虽可即时提交当前值，但会覆盖旧值，造成不可逆错误和历史丢失。因此，亟需一种既能即时提交最新状态，又能保留被替换信息的机制，以兼顾有效性与可追溯性。

Method: 提出并形式化了"Memory of Memory"（MoM）概念，将其具体实现为Provenant Memory（P-Mem）——一种基于类型化溯源图的记忆结构。该系统不仅存储内容，还记录每条记录的来源、状态与历史演变。通过定义一系列类型操作（如支持、取代、反驳、撤销、解决等），对新输入与已有记录进行语义级判断，从而决定如何处理。系统维护一个活跃前沿（active frontier），只暴露每个键的当前有效值，而被取代的旧值则作为溯源信息保留在图中。此外，利用图结构指导回合剪枝，减少无效计算。

Result: P-Mem在多项指标上优于现有方法：在读取精度方面，仅需约1/4的读取令牌即可达到最强检索记忆的准确率，体现显著的粒度优势；在知识更新稳定性方面，过时回答率从19.4%降至10.9%；在修订链任务中，正确率稳定在100%，而传统方法降至25%；最关键的是，在错误提交场景下，其可完全恢复被覆盖的状态（100%恢复率），而传统CRUD记忆无法恢复（0%恢复率）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Provenant Memory（P-Mem）通过引入记忆的溯源机制，实现了写入时承诺当前值的同时保留被取代的历史信息，从根本上解决了长时程智能体中记忆的时效性与可追溯性矛盾。该方法不仅提升了系统对动态知识的响应质量，还增强了容错能力和可解释性，为下一代自主智能体的记忆架构提供了新的范式。

Abstract: For a long-horizon LLM agent, the memory question is not what was once recorded but what \emph{currently holds}. Most designs answer it only indirectly: every interaction is stored, and the present is reconstructed at query time by retrieving and reconciling records, so stale values re-enter and the same conflicts are re-litigated. Committing the current value at write time avoids this, but existing write-time (CRUD) memories overwrite, so a wrong update is unrecoverable and prior state is lost. We take the missing combination---\emph{commit on arrival while retaining what is displaced}---and formalize it as \textsc{Memory of Memory} (MoM): memory tracks not only content but the provenance, status, and history of its own entries. We instantiate MoM as \textsc{Provenant Memory} (P-Mem), a typed provenance graph whose \emph{active frontier} exposes one current value per resolved key while displaced values are retained as provenance; typed operations decide whether a new observation supports, supersedes, contests, rejects, revokes, or resolves an existing value. P-Mem's decisive gain is validity rather than accuracy: its turn-level read matches the strongest retrieval memory in accuracy at $\sim$4$\times$ fewer read tokens---a retrieval-granularity effect---while graph-guided turn pruning cuts the knowledge-update stale-answer rate (19.4\%$\rightarrow$10.9\%); on revision chains it stays at 100\% where query-time reading collapses to 25\%, and, because displaced values are retained rather than overwritten, it recovers committed errors a CRUD memory cannot (100\% vs.\ 0\%).

</details>


### [124] [ICDAR2026 Competition on Multimodal Reasoning over Documents in Multiple Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25055)
*Artemis Llabrés,Marc Serra Ortega,Tomàs Ockier,Samuel Ortega Cuadra,Amritpal Singh,Christos Georgakilas,Andrey Barsky,Ernest Valveny,Dimosthenis Karatzas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ICDAR2026 Competition on Multimodal Reasoning over Documents introduced a diverse, multi-domain VQA benchmark across 8 document types, with 20 submissions from 8 teams using various approaches including zero-shot VLMs, OCR-augmented pipelines, agentic systems, and fine-tuned models. Top-performing systems leverage structured reasoning through evidence extraction, retrieval, verification, and orchestration.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To advance document understanding by challenging models to perform complex multimodal reasoning over diverse real-world document types beyond simple visual question answering.

Method: The competition evaluated systems on a new VQA benchmark covering eight domains: business reports, scientific papers, slides, posters, maps, comics, infographics, and engineering drawings. Submissions included zero-shot VLMs, OCR/parser-augmented pipelines, agentic retrieval systems, multi-agent ensembles, and fine-tuned multimodal models.

Result: Top systems outperformed single-pass prompting by integrating structured processes involving evidence extraction, retrieval, verification, and component orchestration. The results highlight the importance of systematic reasoning over raw vision-language capabilities.

Conclusion: Effective document understanding requires more than direct VQA; it demands structured, multi-step reasoning pipelines that integrate multiple components for robust multimodal comprehension.

Abstract: In this report we present results of the ICDAR2026 Competition on Multimodal Reasoning over Documents in Multiple Domains. This competition aimed to advance research in document understanding through the task of Visual Question Answering (VQA). Building upon previous DocVQA benchmarks, this competition introduces challenging reasoning questions over a diverse collection of documents spanning eight domains, including business reports, scientific papers, slides, posters, maps, comics, infographics, and engineering drawings. The competition concluded with 20 valid submissions from 8 teams spanning zero-shot VLMs, OCR and parser-augmented pipelines, agentic retrieval systems, multi-agent ensembles, and fine-tuned multimodal models. The results show that the strongest systems move beyond single-pass prompting and instead rely on structured evidence extraction, retrieval, verification, and orchestration across multiple components.

</details>


### [125] [Graph-Based Inference for Feedback-Driven Word Deduction: A Scalable Framework for the Jotto Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25056)
*Dakshi Arora,Prakhar Kumar Srivastava,Ranjib Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于反馈的词汇推断框架，用于解决Jotto问题。该框架将问题空间表示为加权图，其中所有有效词汇为节点，边权重由两词之间的共有字母数定义。通过反馈进行迭代约束传播，逐步缩小不相容空间，从而结构化且可解释地减少假设空间。该方法突破了传统固定长度无重复字母的限制，首次统一处理可变长度（3-8字母）和含重复字母的现实Jotto实例。在约3000个模拟游戏场景中，发现迭代次数随词长增加而减少，统计检验确认其存在对数关系，并通过回归模型和拟合度测试验证。此外，该框架引入图剪枝作为反馈驱动推理的新范式，具有可解释性，与符号推理及交互式智能系统相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常局限于固定长度的无重复字母词汇，难以处理现实中的可变长度和含重复字母的Jotto问题。因此，需要一种更通用、可扩展且具可解释性的框架来应对真实场景下的词汇推断挑战。

Method: 将问题空间建模为加权图，其中节点代表有效词汇，边权重表示两词间共有字母数量；利用反馈信息进行迭代约束传播，动态缩减不兼容词汇空间；通过图剪枝实现高效推理，并结合交互式实现与案例研究评估性能与动态行为。

Result: 在约3000个模拟游戏中验证了框架的有效性；发现预期迭代次数随词长增加而下降，统计分析证实存在对数关系，回归模型与拟合度测试支持该结论；框架具备良好的泛化能力，适用于可变长度和重复字母情况，且推理过程具有可解释性。

Conclusion: 所提出的反馈驱动词汇推断框架不仅首次统一处理了现实中的复杂Jotto问题，还揭示了词长与收敛速度之间的对数关系，展示了图剪枝作为可解释反馈推理范式的潜力，为符号推理与交互式智能系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: A feedback-based word deduction framework based on the Jotto problem is proposed, and the problem space is represented as a weighted graph where all valid words correspond to nodes, and the edge weight is defined by the number of common letters between the two words. Finally, the gameplay is defined as an iterative constraint propagation mechanism where feedback is used to iteratively narrow the incompatible space of the graph, facilitating the reduction of the hypothesis space in a structured and interpretable manner.
  In contrast to existing approaches, where the problem space is typically defined for fixed-length isograms, the proposed framework generalizes to variable-length words (between 3 and 8 letters) and naturally extends to repeated letter cases, facilitating the treatment of realistic Jotto problem instances within a unified framework for the first time. The proposed framework's applicability and solver dynamics are also discussed through an interactive implementation and a qualitative case study, respectively.
  Significant automated tests on approximately 3,000 simulated gameplay scenarios identify a novel convergence behavior: the expected number of iterations diminishes with increasing word length. A strong relationship is confirmed using statistical tests to verify a logarithmic relationship, which is also verified using regression modeling and goodness-of-fit tests.
  In addition to the initial problem statement, this formulation introduces graph pruning as a viable paradigm for feedback-driven inference with interpretability and its association with symbolic reasoning and interactive intelligent systems.

</details>


### [126] [ChainDoRA: Tensor-Train Factorized Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation for Parameter-Efficient LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25058)
*Ashfak Yeafi,Mehedi Hasan,Md Khairul Islam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ChainDoRA是一种权重分解的适配框架，通过连接的张量列车（TT）链构建方向性低秩因子，实现了在极低参数量下提升大语言模型的常识推理性能。相比LoRA和DoRA，其在7个基准上平均准确率更高，且参数量减少超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法如LoRA和DoRA虽能减少参数更新，但其方向性分支仍使用密集低秩分解，导致参数开销较大；本文旨在进一步降低参数成本，同时保持或提升下游任务表现。

Method: 提出ChainDoRA，利用张量列车（TT）结构构造方向性低秩因子，其中适配器秩作为输入与输出侧TT收缩的边界秩，独立的TT秩控制表示能力和参数成本，实现参数高效且可调控的权重分解。

Result: 在LLaMA-7B模型上，使用15,119个样本进行响应仅微调时，ChainDoRA（TT rank=16）在7个常识推理任务上平均准确率达72.30%，优于LoRA（69.88%）和DoRA（69.39%），同时仅需5.35M可训练参数，相较DoRA减少90.62%。

Conclusion: ChainDoRA通过连接式张量列车参数化，在显著降低参数量的同时，有效提升了大语言模型在常识推理任务上的性能，证明了其在参数效率与性能之间的优越平衡。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) adapts large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks while updating only a small fraction of their pretrained parameters. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) uses two trainable low-rank matrices, while Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA) further separates weight magnitude and direction but retains the dense LoRA-style factorization in its directional branch. We propose ChainDoRA, a weight-decomposed adaptation framework that constructs the directional low-rank factors from a connected Tensor-Train (TT) chain, where the adapter rank forms the boundary rank between input- and output-side TT contractions and an independent TT rank controls representation capacity and parameter cost. Under a controlled 15,119-example response-only adaptation setting with LLaMA-7B, ChainDoRA is evaluated against matched LoRA and DoRA baselines on seven commonsense reasoning benchmarks. ChainDoRA with TT rank 16 achieves a seven-task average accuracy of 72.30%, compared with 69.88% for LoRA and 69.39% for DoRA, while requiring only 5.35M trainable parameters versus 56.10M for LoRA and 56.98M for DoRA, corresponding to a 90.62% reduction relative to DoRA. Ablations over TT rank and adapter placement show controllable parameter-accuracy trade-offs, indicating that connected TT parameterization can substantially reduce the parameter cost of magnitude-direction adaptation while preserving, and in this setting improving, downstream reasoning performance.

</details>


### [127] [Understanding Reliability in LLM-based Human Behavior Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25066)
*Pei Wang,Lei Wang,Yuanzi Li,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReliMap提出一种三层结构框架，评估大语言模型在人类行为模拟中的可靠性，发现个体与群体层面的可靠性可能背离，需协同优化各层以实现可靠模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法仅关注整体表现，未能揭示模拟过程中各环节如何相互作用影响可靠性，因此需要更细致的分析框架。

Method: 将LLM模拟分为三个结构化层级，从个体（R1）和群体（R2）两个层面，在模型能力、资料完整性和群体覆盖三个维度上进行评估。

Result: 无资料条件时所有模型均存在显著分布偏差；资料条件可降低偏差但收益递减；大模型获益更大，属性信息质量比数量更重要；个体可靠性提升不必然带来群体可靠性提升；群体层面增加覆盖可降低方差但无法消除系统性偏差，R2在50-100人左右趋于稳定。

Conclusion: 可靠的模拟不能仅通过优化单一层次实现，必须协调改进三个层次。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate human survey responses and behavioral reactions, yet unreliable simulations can mislead social science conclusions. However, existing evaluations focus on end-to-end scores, leaving it unclear how different aspects of the simulation process interact to determine reliability. We propose ReliMap, which decomposes LLM-based human behavior simulation into three structured layers and evaluates reliability at both the individual level (R1) and population level (R2) across three configuration dimensions: model capacity, profile completeness, and population coverage. Through experiments across four simulation tasks and eleven LLMs, we find that all models exhibit substantial distributional bias without profile conditioning. Profile conditioning reduces this bias with diminishing returns. Larger models benefit more, and attribute informativeness matters more than quantity. Critically, R1 gains do not reliably transfer to R2--individual and population-level reliability can move in opposite directions. At the population layer, increasing coverage reduces variance but not systematic bias, with R2 stabilizing at around 50-100 individuals. These findings highlight that reliable simulation cannot be achieved by optimizing any single layer in isolation, but requires coordinated improvement across all three.

</details>


### [128] [ufakzeka-1: Building and Evaluating a 151M-Parameter Turkish Language Model from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25081)
*Sait Furkan Teke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ufakzeka-1 是一个151M参数的土耳其语解码器模型，从头开始在135亿个标记的公开许可文本上预训练，并针对聊天任务进行指令微调，总成本约286美元。其贡献不在于模型能力，而在于构建和评估过程的透明记录：包括1.77个标记/词的土耳其语字节级分词器、三阶段预训练计划、后训练阶段混合公开数据与生成数据，以及包含释放门、5508次对话的规则检查扫描、人工评判和手测的评估体系。所有提示均从训练数据中剥离，通过数据构建中的去污染和检查脚本确保一致性。研究发现：安全门被训练数据“修复”后表现理想（64/64），但真实情况仅为34/64；训练种子差异的影响甚至超过不同训练方法的差异，单种子比较在此规模下无意义；长上下文身份追踪和多轮算术任务未因数据变化而改善，表明这是模型大小的限制而非数据缺陷，这一判断将由更大模型验证。权重、数据配方、评估代码和支出账目均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在小规模语言模型中实现高透明度、可复现性和严格评估，特别是在土耳其语等资源较少的语言上建立可靠基准，同时揭示模型训练中潜在的偏差和局限性。

Method: 采用从头预训练方式，使用135亿个标记的公开许可文本，设计了三阶段预训练流程，引入字节级分词器（1.77 tokens per word），结合公开与生成数据进行后训练，构建了包含释放门、规则检查、人工评估和手测的多层评估体系，并通过去污染机制和不变性检查脚本保障数据与评估的纯净性。

Result: 安全门测试显示虚假性能（64/64）远高于真实水平（34/64）；训练种子差异对结果影响极大，甚至超过不同训练方案的差异；长上下文身份追踪和多轮算术任务在所有数据变更下均无提升，表明当前模型容量是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了在小型模型开发中透明性、评估严谨性和数据质量的重要性；发现训练偏差、种子敏感性和模型容量限制是关键挑战，未来更大模型将用于验证这些发现。

Abstract: We describe ufakzeka-1, a 151M-parameter (182M with embeddings) decoder-only Turkish language model pretrained from scratch on 13.5B tokens of openly licensed text and instruction-tuned for chat, at a total cost of about \$286 in cloud GPU, API and notebook time. The contribution is not the model's capability, which is what a model this size can be expected to have, but the record of building and measuring it: a Turkish byte-level tokenizer at 1.77 tokens per word, a three-stage pretraining schedule, a post-training mixture of openly licensed and generated data, and an evaluation battery of release gates, a rule-checked sweep of 5,508 conversations, judged conversations and hand tests, all with prompts held out from the training data, enforced by decontamination inside the data build and by a checked-in invariant script we run before each build.
