### [161] [Damage Predicts Recovery: When Calibration Data Matters in Compressing Financial LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26241)
*Junyi Ye,Mengjia Yu,Debapriya Hazra,Guiling Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了金融领域中后训练量化与剪枝技术对模型性能的影响，重点探讨是否需要使用领域匹配的校准数据。研究发现，是否需要专用校准数据取决于压缩带来的任务级损伤程度：若压缩基本保持目标能力，则校准数据的选择影响不大；若压缩造成显著性能下降，则使用特定任务格式的校准数据（如FinMix）可有效恢复性能。实验在两个模型家族、六种压缩配置、三种匹配校准语料和十项金融任务上验证了该假设，结果支持结论。建议先评估压缩造成的任务损失，仅当损失较大时才构建专门校准数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于金融等专业领域是否需要领域匹配的校准数据尚无定论。由于校准数据通常有限且成本高，需明确何时真正需要专用数据，以提高效率和实用性。

Method: 在两个模型家族上，对六种压缩配置（量化与剪枝）进行测试，采用三种不同来源的校准语料（包括一个金融任务混合数据集FinMix），评估其在十项金融分类与数值问答任务上的表现，并对比不同校准数据对性能的影响。通过测量任务性能变化，分析压缩造成的损伤与校准数据类型之间的关系。

Result: 量化对任务性能影响较小，校准数据选择对其影响不大；而剪枝导致数值问答准确率下降超过40点。在此严重受损情况下，通用校准数据无法缓解损失，但使用金融任务混合数据（FinMix）能显著恢复性能。该现象在不同模型家族和规模下均成立。

Conclusion: 是否需要专用校准数据应基于压缩造成的任务损伤程度决定。若损伤小，无需特殊校准数据；若损伤大，则应构建针对任务特性的校准数据。这一原则具有广泛适用性，有助于优化资源分配与模型部署效率。

Abstract: Post-training quantization and pruning rely on a small calibration corpus. Whether specialized domains such as finance require domain-matched calibration data remains unsettled. We argue that the answer depends on the task-level damage caused by compression rather than on domain mismatch. If compression preserves the target capability, changing the calibration corpus has little effect. If compression causes large losses, task-formatted calibration can recover part of the loss. We test this hypothesis across two model families, six compression configurations, three token-matched calibration corpora, and ten financial classification and numerical question-answering tasks. The results support this hypothesis. Quantization largely preserves task performance, and calibration choice has little effect in this case. Pruning reduces numerical QA accuracy by over 40 points. In these damaged settings, another generic corpus does not help, while FinMix, a mixture of financial task examples, recovers a large part of the loss. The link between damage and recovery holds across model families and scales. These findings support a practical rule. Measure task-specific compression damage first, and construct specialized calibration data only when the damage is large.

</details>


### [162] [PACE-dLLM: Elastic Block Decoding via Confidence Cliff Estimation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26249)
*Xiaocheng Lu,Shuhan Guo,Ziyue Ma,Jie Zhang,Jian Liu,Jingcai Guo,Haoxuan Che,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PACE-dLLM提出一种基于模型自身置信度的新型加速方法，通过拟合每步置信度的分段悬崖曲线，动态确定最优预测视野（look-ahead horizon），并独立设置置信阈值进行高置信度词元提交。该方法在不牺牲生成质量的前提下，显著提升扩散语言模型的推理效率，在多个推理与代码基准上实现最高平均准确率和高达8.52倍的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型（dLLMs）虽具备并行解码优势，但标准的块式解码策略中，预测块长B同时决定前瞻视野和提交词元数量，导致性能受限。当前加速方法依赖间接启发式规则（如波动性跟踪、分隔符检测等），缺乏对内在置信度信息的有效利用。本文旨在挖掘模型自身置信度所蕴含的决策信号，实现更精准、高效的动态加速。

Method: 提出PACE-dLLM框架，通过在每一步拟合置信度的分段悬崖函数（cliff function），以饱和点确定最优展望窗口；同时采用独立的置信阈值判断是否提交词元。该方法基于‘饱和收益’抽象，证明了该悬崖锚定的视野是达到最大单位前向传递有效产出的最小视野。

Result: 在四个推理与代码生成基准测试中，PACE-dLLM在开源dLLM模型上均取得最佳平均准确率，相比未加速的半自回归基线，实现5.23倍（LLaDA）和3.06倍（Dream）的平均壁钟加速，数学任务最高达8.52倍，显著推进了生成质量与吞吐量的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 本研究证明，扩散语言模型自身的置信度分布隐含了最优解码决策信息。通过直接建模置信度的分段悬崖特性，可实现高效、无监督、高质量的动态加速，为dLLMs的实用化部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (dLLMs), such as LLaDA and Dream, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) LLMs in generation quality while supporting native parallel decoding. A standard acceleration strategy is block-wise decoding, where each forward pass predicts a block of length B and commits high-confidence tokens. However, B couples two distinct decisions: the look-ahead horizon and the number of tokens to commit. Existing accelerators address this limitation through indirect heuristics, such as volatility tracking, delimiter detection, and learned scoring. In contrast, we show that the required information is already encoded in the model's own per-step confidence: in-window confidence typically follows a context-dependent cliff, whose saturation point directly identifies the appropriate look-ahead horizon. We propose PACE-dLLM, which fits this parametric cliff in closed form at each step, sets the next horizon by its saturation point, and uses an independent confidence threshold for token commitment. Under a saturated-yield abstraction, we show that the cliff-anchored horizon is the smallest horizon attaining maximal useful per-pass yield: fixed horizons that undershoot it incur a worse asymptotic NFE rate, while overshooting adds no useful yield. On four reasoning and code benchmarks, PACE-dLLM achieves the best average accuracy on both open-source dLLM backbones, with average wall-clock speedups of 5.23x on LLaDA and 3.06x on Dream (up to 8.52x on math) over the unaccelerated semi-AR baseline, advancing the quality-throughput Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [163] [CHiME-9 ECHI: A Machine Learning Challenge for Enhancing Conversations to Address Hearing Impairment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26306)
*Robert Sutherland,Thomas Kuebert,Marko Lugger,Stefan Petrausch,Eline Borch Petersen,Juan Azcarreta Ortiz,Buye Xu,Stefan Goetze,Jon Barker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CHiME-9挑战赛聚焦于在嘈杂的咖啡馆环境中增强四人对话的语音，旨在提升听力障碍者的语音可懂度和质量。使用Meta Aria眼镜和助听器麦克风采集的多通道音频与干净语音样本作为输入，目标是从噪声中提取对话者语音。七支队伍参与，通过客观指标和主观听感测试评估性能。结果显示，尽管客观指标未能准确反映听觉表现，但最佳系统在可懂度和音质方面均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决听力障碍者在嘈杂环境（如咖啡馆）中进行多人对话时面临的语音识别困难问题，提升语音可懂度和感知质量。

Method: 基于多通道音频记录，利用参与者提交的算法从包含干扰语音和背景声的复杂环境中分离出目标说话人语音，并结合客观评估指标与主观听感测试进行综合评价。

Result: 尽管客观指标与主观表现不完全一致，但最优系统在可懂度和音质方面均明显优于基线，表明方法具有实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: CHiME-9挑战赛验证了在真实复杂场景下提升对话语音质量的有效性，为听力辅助技术的发展提供了重要参考。

Abstract: This work presents the task and results of the CHiME-9 challenge for Enhancing Conversations to address Hearing Impairment. The challenge considers the scenario of four-party conversations in a noisy, cafeteria-style environment with interfering speech sources and sound effects. Participants are provided with audio recordings made with Meta Aria glasses and hearing aid microphones, and clean speech samples of the conversation participants. The task is to extract the speech of the conversation partners from the noisy multi-channel recordings with the goal of improving the intelligibility and quality of the speech, evaluated using objective metrics and subjective listening tests. This paper reviews submissions from seven teams and ranks them on a combination of subjective intelligibility and quality. Results show that while the objective metrics do not reflect listener performance, the top systems were able to make substantial improvements over the challenge baseline in both intelligibility and quality ratings.

</details>


### [164] [Designing and Analysing Argument Mining Pipelines: Towards a Comprehensive Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26338)
*Siddharth Bhargava,Sara Tonelli,Patricia Martín-Rodilla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种初步的元研究，系统回顾了多个最先进的端到端论点挖掘（AM）工作，并通过语言学、计算和领域三个视角的框架分析其处理流程，以理解这些流程如何建模论点结构、进行计算以及整合领域知识。文章还提出了一个通用设计，用于语言学和计算视角，展示关键AM任务如何被设计来建模和计算论点结构。该框架为方法学为中心的描述提供了基础，有助于未来研究中更深入的理解和系统性比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前论点挖掘方法在任务概念化上存在差异，导致不同方法之间的直接比较困难且不透明，因此需要一种更细致的任务层面分析来促进清晰的比较与评估。

Method: 采用三重视角框架（语言学、计算、领域）对多个先进的端到端论点挖掘工作进行系统性回顾与分析，并提出一个通用的设计方案以统一语言学和计算视角中的关键任务设计。

Result: 成功构建了一个能够支持方法学为中心描述的框架，为未来论点挖掘研究提供系统性比较与深入理解的基础。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为论点挖掘方法的标准化描述与比较提供了坚实基础，有助于推动该领域的规范化发展。

Abstract: Argument Mining (AM) transforms natural language into its underlying argument structures. This transformation is typically realized through a sequence of AM tasks that form an end-to-end AM pipeline. However, AM approaches often differ in how they conceptualize these tasks, making direct comparisons between them difficult and opaque. This calls for a more nuanced, task-level analysis of AM approaches to enable clearer comparison and assessment.
  This work presents a preliminary meta-study that systematically reviews several state-of-the-art end-to-end AM works and analyzes their pipelines through a triple-perspective framework---a linguistic, computational and domain perspective---to understand how the pipelines model arguments as structures, computes them, and integrates domain knowledge. We further propose a general design to the linguistic and computational perspectives, illustrating how key AM tasks are designed for modeling and computation of argument structures. Our proposed framework lays the groundwork for methodology-centered descriptions across AM approaches, facilitating deeper understanding and more systematic comparisons in future research.

</details>


### [165] [Blaming Across the Aisle: Political Contrasting and Blame Attribution in the Danish Parliament](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26346)
*Markus Lundsfryd Jensen,Rune Egeskov Trust,Kenneth Christian Enevoldsen,Sara Kolding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过构建针对低至中等资源语言的责备归属分类器BlameBERT（F1: 0.80）并结合多层次统计建模，分析了丹麦议会1997至2026年间的责备话语。结果显示责备行为呈香蕉形轨迹，先下降至约2016年，随后在2019-2026年间显著且持续上升。执政地位显著影响责备分配，形成所谓‘政治对比’效应：反对党比执政党更频繁地责备他人。该效应受意识形态调节：右翼政党执政时责备抑制效应较弱，且极端意识形态在右翼中加剧责备行为。近年来，政治阵营与意识形态极端性的交互作用增强，表明责备话语在右翼政治光谱中出现意识形态固化。整体而言，政治话语的“恶化”并非普遍修辞漂移，而是具有意识形态不对称性的强化趋势。敏感性分析显示结论对分类阈值变化稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治话语被认为日益敌对，但缺乏坚实证据。本研究旨在通过量化分析丹麦议会中的责备话语，揭示其长期演变模式及背后的结构性机制，特别是执政地位、意识形态和时间趋势之间的相互作用，以检验公众对政治敌意上升的感知是否具有实证基础。

Method: 采用自建的责备归属分类器BlameBERT，基于注释高效的流程处理低至中等资源语言；结合多层统计模型分析1997至2026年丹麦议会文本数据，考察责备行为随时间、政党身份和意识形态的变化规律，并进行敏感性分析验证结果稳健性。

Result: 责备行为呈现香蕉形轨迹，先降后升，2019-2026年显著增加；执政党责备明显少于反对党，形成‘政治对比’效应；右翼政党执政时责备抑制效应减弱，极端意识形态在右翼中更强地激发责备行为；近年来政治阵营与极端主义的交互作用增强，体现右翼话语的意识形态固化。

Conclusion: 政治话语的敌意上升并非普遍修辞漂移，而是一种具有意识形态不对称性的结构性强化，尤其体现在右翼政治力量中，反映了政治话语的意识形态分化与激化趋势。

Abstract: Political discourse is widely perceived to be growing more hostile, yet robust evidence remains scarce. This study examines blame attribution in the Danish Parliament from 1997 to 2026, combining a purpose-built classifier, BlameBERT (F1: 0.80), with multilevel statistical modeling. The classifier is constructed using an annotation-efficient pipeline for blame attribution in low-to-mid resource languages. The results reveal a banana-shaped trajectory, with blame declining until around 2016 before entering a significant and sustained increase in recent years (2019-2026). Government status consistently influenced blame attribution - an effect we term political contrasting - with opposition parties blaming substantially more than governing parties. This effect was moderated by ideology: The blame-dampening effect of governing was less pronounced among right-wing parties, and ideological extremity amplified blame more strongly on the right. In recent years, the interaction between political wing and ideological extremity intensified, suggesting an ideological hardening of the blame rhetoric concentrated on the right of the political spectrum. Taken together, these patterns suggest that the perceived rise in harsh political language reflects not merely a general rhetorical drift, but an ideologically asymmetric hardening of political discourse. A sensitivity analysis showed that the conclusions were robust to varying classification thresholds.

</details>


### [166] [TransBERT: A Framework for Synthetic Translation in Domain-Specific Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26347)
*Julien Knafou,Luc Mottin,Anaïs Mottaz,Alexandre Flament,Patrick Ruch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TransBERT框架和TransCorpus工具包，通过合成翻译构建法语生命科学领域的高质量NLP资源，证明了在高资源翻译方向上使用合成数据可实现顶尖性能，释放了36.4GB的法语生物医学语料库、预训练模型及可复现代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非英语专业领域数据稀缺严重制约NLP工具发展，尤其在低资源语言-领域组合中更显不足，亟需有效解决方案以提升多语言NLP能力。

Method: 采用合成翻译方法构建大规模法语生命科学文本数据集，利用TransCorpus工具包进行高效生成，并基于此预训练TransBERT-bio-fr语言模型，实现下游任务的高性能表现。

Result: 在多个下游任务中达到当前最优水平，验证了仅依赖合成翻译数据即可构建高质量低资源语言-领域NLP资源的可行性。

Conclusion: 合成翻译在高资源翻译方向上具有显著潜力，为低资源语言与专业领域提供了一条可行的NLP资源建设路径。

Abstract: The scarcity of non-English language data in specialized domains significantly limits the development of effective Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. We present TransBERT, a novel framework for pre-training language models using exclusively synthetically translated text, and introduce TransCorpus, a scalable translation toolkit. Focusing on the life sciences domain in French, our approach demonstrates that state-of-the-art performance on various downstream tasks can be achieved solely by leveraging synthetically translated data. We release the TransCorpus toolkit, the TransCorpus-bio-fr corpus (36.4GB of French life sciences text), TransBERT-bio-fr, its associated pre-trained language model and reproducible code for both pre-training and fine-tuning. Our results highlight the viability of synthetic translation in a high-resource translation direction for building high-quality NLP resources in low-resource language/domain pairs.

</details>


### [167] [HySparse2: Hybrid Sparse Attention with Two-Level KV Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26368)
*Jianyu Wei,Yizhao Gao,Qihao Zhang,Shimao Chen,Zhengju Tang,Yu Cheng,Shengjie Zhou,Zihan Jiang,Yifan Song,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Bo Yang,Gang Wang,Shijie Cao,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HySparse2是一种两级KV共享的混合稀疏注意力架构，通过优化预填充和长上下文检索，在保持性能的同时显著减少计算和存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长时序、多轮对话代理在处理长观察和短动作时面临的上下文管理挑战，包括高效的预填充、紧凑的KV缓存存储和精确的长上下文检索。

Method: 采用两层KV共享机制：外层使用自解码器与交叉解码器结构，其中自解码器采用混合滑动窗口注意力，交叉解码器采用混合稀疏注意力，并利用自解码器中全注意力层的隐藏状态生成交叉解码器的KV缓存；内层改进了原有的块级稀疏为逐标记稀疏，并将最近的标记强制纳入稀疏选择中，实现更精细的长上下文检索。所有交叉解码器的KV缓存均由自解码器隐藏状态构建，从而可在自解码器完成后立即退出预填充过程，跳过所有交叉解码器层。

Result: 在80B-A3B MoE模型上，HySparse2在长上下文检索和多轮代理任务中优于HySparse和Hybrid SWA，同时大幅降低预填充计算量和KV缓存存储需求。

Conclusion: HySparse2通过两级KV共享设计，实现了高效预填充、低存储开销与高精度长上下文检索的平衡，适用于大规模长时序多轮交互场景。

Abstract: Long-horizon and multi-turn agents typically generate short actions and process long observations from tools and environments. This growing context demands efficient prefill, compact KV-cache storage, and accurate long-context retrieval. To meet these demands, we introduce HySparse2, a hybrid sparse attention architecture with two-level KV sharing. At the outer level, KV Bridging adopts a YOCO-style self-decoder and cross-decoder structure, but bridges only full-attention layers. The self-decoder uses hybrid sliding-window attention (SWA), while the cross-decoder uses hybrid sparse attention. The KV caches for full-attention layers in the cross-decoder are generated from the hidden states of full-attention layers in the self-decoder. At the inner level, HySparse2 retains HySparse's core KV Reuse design with two refinements. First, it replaces block-level sparsity with token-level sparsity for finer long-context retrieval. Second, it removes the separate SWA branch from sparse layers and instead forces a sliding window of recent tokens into the sparse selection. This two-level KV sharing allows all cross-decoder KV caches to be constructed from self-decoder hidden states. Prefill can therefore exit after the self-decoder, skipping all cross-decoder layers. On an 80B-A3B MoE model, HySparse2 outperforms HySparse and Hybrid SWA on long-context retrieval and multi-turn agentic tasks, while substantially reducing prefill computation and KV-cache storage.

</details>


### [168] [Layout-Guided Masking for GROBID: Lightweight Structural Gains in Large-Scale Scientific PDF Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26381)
*Luca Foppiano,Sana Khamassi,Vipul Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于CPU的轻量级检测器，与GROBID结合，用于将学术PDF转换为机器可读的全文。该方法通过类型化区域掩码定位图表、表格和页眉页脚区域，并将相应文本路由至GROBID的专用模型或丢弃，显著提升结构化准确性。在PMC数据集上，其性能优于纯GROBID，在段落召回率、章节检测和图注关联方面均有提升；在外部表检测基准上，F1分数从0.16提升至0.94。相比多个视觉模型，该方法在段落精度和章节识别上表现最佳，字符错误率接近最强GPU系统。整个流程运行于CPU，成本仅为最便宜GPU系统的1/3至1/14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉解析器依赖GPU，成本高且可能引入噪声；而传统解析工具如GROBID虽高效但缺乏对图表、表格等复杂元素的精准定位能力。因此需要一种低成本、高性能、无需GPU的解决方案，以实现大规模学术文献结构化处理。

Method: 提出一个轻量级的CPU运行检测器，用于识别图表、表格及页眉页脚等区域，生成带有类型的区域掩码。这些掩码指导文本流如何被分发至GROBID的不同模块进行处理，或直接丢弃非正文内容。整个系统端到端运行于CPU，避免了对昂贵硬件的需求。

Result: 在两个PMC数据集（生物信息学与材料科学）上，该方法在多数指标上优于原始GROBID，尤其在段落召回率（+0.086）、章节检测和图注匹配方面表现突出；在Table-BRGM基准上，表检测F1从0.16升至0.94，结构提取性能也大幅提升。与四个主流视觉系统相比，该方法在段落精度和章节识别上领先，字符错误率仅比最优GPU系统高出0.004。全程运行于CPU，能耗成本仅为最便宜GPU系统的1/3至1/14。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构建了一个高效、低成本、全运行于CPU的学术文献结构化系统，兼具高精度与可扩展性，适用于大规模开放科学数据基础设施建设。

Abstract: Transforming scholarly PDFs into machine-readable fulltext remains a bottleneck for large-scale information systems. Recent vision-based parsers improve accuracy, but need GPUs and may introduce noise into the extracted text. GROBID, a modular font-stream parser running on CPU, is the de-facto standard for structuring scientific articles and underpins several of the largest open scholarly corpora. We pair it with a lightweight CPU detector localising figure, table, and paratext (header, footer, page number) regions, encoded as typed-area masks whose tokens are routed to GROBID's specialised models or discarded. On two PMC corpora, Bioinformatics (1,926 articles) and Materials Science (2,595), scored against JATS with a section-aware structural protocol, our extension improves over plain GROBID on most metrics (NS $+0.025$/$+0.013$; $+0.086$ paragraph recall on Materials Science, $d_z{=}1.08$), and caption-linked figure recovery improves on both corpora. On the external Table-BRGM benchmark, table detection recovers F1 $0.16 \to 0.94$ and table structure follows (GriTS-Top $0.27 \to 0.78$, below the strongest GPU system). On body text, against four vision-based systems (Docling, MinerU, olmOCR, dots.ocr), it has the best paragraph precision on both corpora, the best section detection on Materials Science, and a character error rate within 0.004 of the best GPU parser. End-to-end on CPU, it costs $2.7$--$3.2\times$ less than the cheapest GPU system (Docling) and $10$--$14\times$ less than generative parsers.

</details>


### [169] [Combining Hierarchical Cognitive Process with Process Supervision for Interpretable Scene Safety Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26399)
*Zhiyun Jiang,Hanyong Wang,Binbin Liang,Yu Xie,Zhengjie Wang,Menglong Yang,Wei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于人类认知过程建模的场景安全理解框架，通过构建分层认知安全结构并建立包含多步推理过程标签的高质量数据集，提升大语言模型（LLM）在安全推理中的能力。采用模块化设计，结合LoRA与MoE策略，实现专家模块在不同子任务上的专业化协作，显著增强模型的可解释性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖直接映射场景与安全等级，缺乏可解释性，难以在关键应用中保证可靠性。为提高安全性与可信度，需模拟人类认知过程，增强模型的理解与推理能力。

Method: 构建分层认知安全结构，创建带有过程标签的多步推理数据集；基于大语言模型，设计模块化、灵活的过程监督框架，融合LoRA与Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）技术，实现专家模块的分工与协同。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在可解释性和性能上均优于传统方法，能够有效支持对中间推理步骤的精细分析，并提升安全理解能力。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类认知过程并引入过程监督机制，本研究实现了更可靠、可解释的场景安全理解，为关键领域中的智能系统提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Scene safety understanding plays a life-or-death role in situational awareness in various critical domains. Traditional methods that rely on learning direct mappings between scenes and safety levels often lack interpretability, limiting their reliability in critical applications. An effective approach to overcoming this challenge lies in interpreting human cognitive processes and equipping machine models with analogous cognitive capabilities. This work explores an effective way of integrating scene safety cognitive process modeling and process supervision. Specifically, we first construct a hierarchical cognitive safety structure, which motivates the development of a novel, high-quality scene safety understanding dataset based on multi-step reasoning with process labels. This dataset serves both as a benchmark and a resource to improve the safety reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), while also enabling a granular analysis of intermediate reasoning steps through information flow and saliency-based techniques. Building upon this foundation, we introduce a modular and flexible process supervision framework that reflects the hierarchical nature of human cognition. This framework leverages LLMs as the core architecture and incorporates Low-Rank Adaptation(LoRA) and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) strategies to enable specialization and collaboration among expert modules, each tasked with specific sub-processes of the overall reasoning chain. Systematic experimental evaluations and analyses confirm that our framework exhibits superior interpretability and performance characteristics compared to traditional approaches.

</details>


### [170] [Enriching Speech Emotion Representations with Conversational Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26422)
*Arthur Peuvot,Romaric Besançon,Gaël de Chalendar,Bianca Vieru,Ioana Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ACERT是一种用于语音情感识别的模块，通过引入灵活长度的对话上下文窗口，更好地捕捉口语交互中的情感演变。在IEMOCAP、SAFE和MELD等多个数据集上，ACERT表现优于现有最先进方法，尤其在考虑情感连续性和对话连贯性方面效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音情感识别研究多集中在单个语句层面，忽视了对话上下文、情感流和说话人互动，导致对情感动态变化的建模不足。为提升情感识别的准确性与适应性，需要引入更丰富的上下文信息。

Method: 提出ACERT（Averaged Contextual Emotion Representation through Time）模块，利用可变长度的对话上下文窗口，融合历史话语的情感表示，实现对情感演化的建模。该模块通过时间平均方式整合上下文信息，增强模型对情感连续性的感知能力。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上超越SOTA方法；在SAFE上建立首个上下文感知基准；在MELD上取得优异的未加权和类别平衡指标结果；消融实验表明性能提升主要源于情感与对话连续性，而非说话人身份或声学条件。

Conclusion: ACERT有效提升了语音情感识别在复杂对话场景下的表现，证明了引入上下文信息对于捕捉情感演化的重要性，为构建更具适应性的智能语音交互系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Detecting emotions is necessary for building systems that can accurately and adaptively interact with humans. Speech Emotion Recognition (SER) has become an important research focus to develop intelligent spoken interfaces. However, most studies predict emotions at the utterance level, ignoring the conversational context, along with the emotional flow and speaker interactions it carries. In this paper, we introduce ACERT (Averaged Contextual Emotion Representation through Time), a module that integrates a flexible-length window of conversational context to better capture emotional evolution in spoken interactions. To evaluate the robustness of this method, we conducted experiments on datasets spanning diverse emotionally expressive styles and contexts. ACERT outperforms current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches on IEMOCAP, establishes the first context-aware benchmark on SAFE, and obtains strong results on MELD for unweighted, class-balanced metrics. Ablation studies show that ACERT's gains come from emotional and conversational continuity, rather than from speaker identity or acoustic conditions.

</details>


### [171] [How to Estimate Whether You Have Found Several Needles in a Haystack: Measuring Calibration in Multi-Label Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26468)
*Sophie Henning,Georg Hofmann,Alexander Schulte,Alexander Fraser,Annemarie Friedrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多标签分类中的置信度校准问题，指出现有基于分箱的校准误差度量在处理大量负标签时存在低估、受标签频率影响或样本过少等问题。为此，提出一种新的分箱方案，使正负标签分配权重相等，从而获得更可靠的标签级校准误差估计。实验表明，该方法在层次化和极端多标签分类任务中表现优异，并揭示大语言模型在多标签预测中的置信度校准仍是一个开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标签分类任务中普遍存在大量负标签，现有校准度量方法在处理此类数据时表现不佳，无法提供准确的校准误差评估，亟需更可靠的评估机制以提升预测可信度。

Method: 提出一种新的分箱方案，通过赋予正负标签分配相同的权重，改进标签级期望校准误差的计算方式，避免因标签频率或稀疏样本导致的偏差。

Result: 所提方法在多种多标签分类场景（包括层次化与极端多标签）中均能产生有意义且可靠的校准误差估计；同时发现大语言模型在多标签预测中的置信度校准仍面临重大挑战。

Conclusion: 本文提出的分箱方法为多标签分类的置信度校准提供了更稳健的评估框架，为后续研究奠定了基础，尤其在高维、稀疏的多标签任务中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: A key factor in deciding whether to trust an automatic prediction is its confidence score, which should be calibrated to match the actual probability of the prediction being correct. Most confidence calibration metrics target binary or multi-class tasks, while multi-label calibration remains largely underexplored. Multi-label classification tasks, such as assigning medical codes to clinical notes or determining news topics, are usually dominated by a large number of negatives, i.e., labels that do not apply. We show that existing binning schemes to compute label-wise expected calibration error either underestimate the error, simply reflect label frequency, or suffer from many bins with very few instances. To achieve trustworthy label-wise calibration errors, we propose a new binning scheme that gives equal weight to positive and negative label assignments. Our empirical study demonstrates that in contrast to existing binning schemes, our new scheme results in meaningful estimates of calibration error in hierarchical and in extreme multi-label classification. We also show that calibrating confidence scores of large language models for multi-label predictions is an open challenge. Our detailed analysis lays the foundation for further research by providing a solid evaluation metric for measuring calibration in multi-label classification.

</details>


### [172] [Spoken Language Models that Think Aloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26488)
*Junyi Ao,Kainan Peng,Mingbo Ma,Shun Zhang,Zhenyu Tang,Xutai Ma,Xiang Li,Yinghao Li,Yuancheng Wang,Zhizheng Wu,Haizhou Li,Qing He,Xubo Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种异步思考-发声框架，用于改进语音语言模型在推理时的实时交互体验。该框架通过主推理流与轻量级发声流的协同，减少沉默间隔，同时保持答案准确性，显著提升了响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接将链式思维（CoT）应用于语音语言模型会因串行‘思考-说话’模式导致长时间沉默，影响实时语音交互体验。

Method: 设计了基于思考者-说话者架构的异步思考-发声框架，包含主推理流和轻量级任务相关进展语音流，并采用动态平衡策略协调两流，以避免沉默间隙并适时取消待发语音。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著减少了推理过程中的用户可感知沉默，同时保持了与串行‘思考-说话’基线相当的答案准确率，证明了其在提升语音语言模型响应性方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 异步思考-发声框架有效缓解了语音语言模型中推理过程的延迟问题，为实现高效、自然的实时语音交互提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has improved the capability of language models, directly applying it to Spoken Language Models (SLMs) may introduce long silent intervals under the serial "think-then-speak" paradigm, disrupting real-time spoken interaction. To address this issue, we propose an asynchronous think-aloud framework for reasoning-based SLMs within the Thinker-Talker architecture. The framework maintains a primary reasoning stream for logical deduction and a lightweight think-aloud stream that generates short, task-grounded progress utterances conditioned on the user input and the evolving reasoning state. A dynamic balance strategy coordinates the two streams at runtime, triggering additional think-aloud speech to avoid silent gaps and canceling pending utterances when the final response becomes ready. Experiments on spoken reasoning and question-answering benchmarks show that our approach substantially reduces user-audible silence during reasoning while maintaining answer accuracy comparable to that of a serial "think-then-speak" baseline, demonstrating the potential of asynchronous think-aloud for responsive interaction in SLMs.

</details>


### [173] [Calibration as a First-Class Criterion in LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26489)
*Mario Sanz-Guerrero,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文强调语言模型校准的重要性，指出尽管已有测量方法，但实际应用中很少被采用。校准缺失导致部署时过度自信的错误造成真实危害，并影响研究流程中的评估方法如LLM-as-a-judge等。论文主张将校准作为每个模型的基本属性，建议各子领域在主性能指标外增加校准得分，并利用现有基准数据立即报告校准情况。开放生成任务中定义置信度和正确性仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型研究中缺乏对置信度校准的重视，导致模型在部署和研究过程中因过度自信而产生问题，阻碍了可信评估的发展。

Method: 提出将校准作为基本评估指标，建议结合现有基准数据直接报告校准分数；针对开放生成任务，探索定义置信度与正确性判断的方法。

Result: 校准可立即应用于多数现有基准，提升模型评估的可靠性；若各子领域引入校准评分，将显著增强模型可信度和研究质量。

Conclusion: 应将校准视为模型的核心属性，而非小众话题，推动其在所有NLP研究中的常规化应用。

Abstract: Calibration of language models -- the alignment between expressed or implicit confidence and empirical correctness -- is a well-studied subfield within NLP. Methods to measure it already exist. The problem is adoption: outside this subfield, NLP research regularly introduces new models, datasets, and benchmarks without checking whether the model's confidence scores are meaningful. We argue that this adoption gap is a major obstacle to trustworthy LLM evaluation. Miscalibration causes problems in two distinct areas: at deployment, where overconfident mistakes cause real harm, and inside the research pipeline, where methods like LLM-as-a-judge, synthetic data generation, and active learning rely on calibrated confidence without verifying it. Standard calibration metrics only require two inputs per example: a confidence score and a correctness judgment. Most benchmarks in use today already provide both, meaning calibration can be reported immediately. For open-ended generation, however, defining these two inputs is still an open challenge. We argue that each NLP subfield should pair its main performance metric with a calibration score and call for treating calibration as an essential property of every model rather than a niche topic.

</details>


### [174] [A Semiotics-Aware Framework for Evaluating Fidelity and Coverage in Natural Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26527)
*Lorenzo Zangari,Davide Picca*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种评估文本间符号对齐的框架，通过语义轮廓分析上下文意义和话语引用，计算出语义保真度和语义覆盖度两个分数，以衡量文本间的对齐程度。实验表明，覆盖度通常低于保真度，且在低采样温度下，大语言模型与人工标注数据的对齐度最高，高温会降低对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的基于词汇重叠或整体文本相似性的度量方法在评估相同表达的不同表述时可能忽略其语境和话语层面的差异，因此需要一种能够捕捉符号层面对齐的新方法。

Method: 提出基于语义轮廓的符号对齐评估框架，定义了语义保真度（衡量一个文本的轮廓在另一个文本中被支持的程度）和语义覆盖度（衡量一个文本恢复另一文本轮廓的程度），利用该框架进行量化分析。

Result: 实验结果显示，语义覆盖度普遍低于语义保真度；大语言模型在低采样温度下与人工标注数据的符号对齐性最佳，而提高采样温度会削弱这种对齐性。

Conclusion: 所提出的符号对齐框架能有效揭示文本间在语义和话语层面的细微差异，有助于评估大语言模型生成内容与人类表达之间的契合程度，尤其在控制采样温度方面具有重要意义。

Abstract: When two texts describe the same expression, standard metrics based on lexical overlap or whole-text similarity may fail to detect meaningful differences in how that expression is framed. We propose a framework to evaluate semiotic alignment between texts, where a semiotic profile encompasses both the contextual meaning and the discourse references made salient by a text. Our approach yields two scores, Semiotic Fidelity and Semiotic Coverage, estimating how much of one text's profile is supported by the other and how much of the other's profile it recovers. Experiments show that coverage is typically lower than fidelity, and that alignment between LLMs and human-curated data is highest at low sampling temperatures, while higher temperatures reduce this alignment.

</details>


### [175] [Transcribe, Translate, and Optimize: Joint Reward Learning for Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26536)
*Yanghe Dong,Wanting Huang,Weiran Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于组相对策略优化（GRPO）的联合语音识别与翻译微调方法，以解决大语言模型在语音翻译中因参考转录与模型生成转录不一致导致的训练-推理失配问题。通过同时评分转录和翻译，并采用三种不同的词优势策略，实验表明该方法在多种语言上显著优于传统监督微调和直接语音翻译方法，提升翻译质量并降低误识率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于大语言模型的语音翻译中，传统的基于转录的思维链（CoT）方法存在训练阶段使用参考转录、推理阶段使用模型生成转录的不一致问题，导致性能下降。因此需要一种能够缓解这一训练-推理失配的新方法。

Method: 提出联合识别与翻译的微调框架，采用组相对策略优化（GRPO），对模型生成的转录及其对应的翻译进行联合评分，同时比较三种不同的词级优势策略，以优化翻译性能。

Result: 在CoVoST 2和FLEURS数据集上的实验显示，CoT GRPO相比Direct ST GRPO分别提升1.77和0.83 BLEU点；相较于CoT SFT，GRPO在两个数据集上分别提升0.82和0.67 BLEU点，并相对降低8.8%和7.2%的词错误率（WER）。

Conclusion: 强化学习微调（如GRPO）是缓解训练-推理失配的有效手段，能够联合提升语音识别与翻译性能，尤其在转录与翻译协同优化方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: In LLM-based speech translation, transcription-based chain-of-thought (CoT) suffers from a mismatch between reference transcripts used in supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and model-generated transcripts at inference. To address this, we propose joint recognition and translation fine-tuning via group relative policy optimization (GRPO). We score both transcripts and translations, with translation conditioned on model-generated transcripts, and compare three token advantage strategies. Using Qwen2.5-Omni-3B across four languages, we evaluate CoT against direct speech translation (Direct ST) under SFT and GRPO, training on CoVoST 2 and testing on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. CoT GRPO outperforms Direct ST GRPO by 1.77 and 0.83 average BLEU points on CoVoST 2 and FLEURS. Compared to CoT SFT, GRPO boosts BLEU by 0.82 and 0.67 points and reduces word error rate (WER) by 8.8% and 7.2% relatively. These results highlight reinforcement fine-tuning as an effective method to mitigate the training-inference mismatch, jointly improving recognition and translation.

</details>


### [176] [A retrospective analysis on the use of LLMs to study infant syntax learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26539)
*Hélie Bazin,Anouk Barberousse,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对大语言模型（LLMs）在婴儿语法习得研究中的应用进行了认识论评估，反思了BabyLM等研究项目的方法学假设，指出其在数据构建、模型选择、训练方式和语法评估上的局限性，并强调发展现实语料对模型表现的影响有限，暗示LLMs与婴儿语法学习者之间存在重要计算差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨大语言模型在模拟婴儿语法习得过程中的有效性与理论适用性，评估BabyLM等研究项目的科学合理性与局限性。

Method: 通过分析多个与婴儿语法学习相关的研究，系统考察数据集构建方式、模型实现、训练策略及语法评估方法，进行方法学与认识论层面的批判性评估。

Result: 发现BabyLM及相关研究存在显著方法学假设，限制了其理论解释力；同时，使用发展现实语料并未显著提升模型在标准基准测试中的表现，表明LLMs与真实婴儿语法学习机制之间存在根本性计算差异。

Conclusion: 尽管大语言模型在模拟早期语法习得方面具有潜力，但当前研究仍受限于方法假设和计算机制不匹配的问题，需更谨慎地解读其结果，并推动更具生物学合理性的建模路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly been used to investigate how children acquire syntax at an early stage of development. This is notably the central scientific goal of the BabyLM challenge, a community-wide effort to develop models that achieve human-level syntactic performance while being trained on developmentally realistic corpora. In this paper, we reflect on the use of LLMs in the study of infant syntax learning by providing an epistemological assessment of several studies from this research program. We discuss how datasets are built, which models are implemented, how they are trained and syntactically evaluated. We observe significant assumptions in the methodology of BabyLM and related studies, thus mitigating their theoretical scope. We additionally observe that using developmentally-realistic corpora have limited effects on models performance on commonly-used benchmarks, which suggest important computational differences between LLMs and the infant syntax learner.

</details>


### [177] [Receptiveness, Not Sycophancy: Distinguishing Engagement from Deference in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26579)
*Calvin Isley,Johann Gaebler,Max Lamparth,Julia Minson,Sharad Goel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨语言模型中的奉承行为（sycophancy），指出社会性奉承的评估指标与对话接受性存在重叠，可能导致对理想沟通行为的误判。研究发现，更具接受性的回复被评作更奉承，且在保持观点不变的情况下提升接受性会增加奉承评分。实验表明，接受性更高的回复更受用户欢迎，更能促进互动和信任。文章提出一种方法，在不增加实质性让步的前提下显著提升接受性，证明了接受性与独立判断可兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将社会性奉承视为语言模型的缺陷，但其衡量标准与心理学中的对话接受性高度重合，导致评估可能误伤有益沟通行为，影响模型设计的合理性。

Method: 基于道德建议数据集，通过人工标注与自动分类分析奉承与接受性的关系；进行预注册实验，比较具有相同实质结论但接受性不同的回复，考察用户偏好、倾听意愿及求助意愿。

Result: 接受性高的回复虽被标记为更奉承，却更受用户欢迎，用户更愿意倾听并寻求其建议；即使对方立场错误，这一趋势依然成立。提出的方法能有效提升接受性而不增加实质妥协。

Conclusion: 社会性奉承的评估存在构念有效性问题，当前指标可能错误地惩罚了有益的对话行为。应区分‘接受性’与‘实质服从’，通过技术手段实现二者兼顾，以提升人机交互质量。

Abstract: A central concern with language models is sycophancy: their tendency to defer to users' views at the expense of independent substantive judgment. In parallel, work on social sycophancy has focused on behaviors such as validation and positivity that may signal inappropriate deference. Yet the markers of social sycophancy are also characteristic of conversational receptiveness, a construct from social psychology shown to improve interactions across disagreement. We argue that this overlap creates a construct-validity problem for social sycophancy evaluations. Using a popular moral-advice dataset, we find that responses classified as more socially sycophantic are also more receptive. Further, increasing the receptiveness of human-written responses---while preserving their substantive conclusions---causes them to be classified as more socially sycophantic. This tight coupling raises the possibility that social sycophancy evaluations inadvertently penalize desirable behavior. In a preregistered experiment comparing substantively equivalent responses, participants prefer the more receptive responses, expect users to be more likely to listen to them, and are more willing to seek advice from their authors. The same overall pattern persists even among participants who believe the original question asker is in the wrong. Finally, we introduce a simple approach that substantially increases receptiveness without increasing substantive deference, demonstrating that conversational receptiveness and substantive independence can be achieved together.

</details>


### [178] [Semantic Abstraction for Natural Language Inference: a Methodological Framework for Discovering and Compensating Semantic Knowledge and Reasoning Gaps in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26610)
*David Torres-Moreno,Jorge Hermosillo-Valadez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种方法论框架，通过重构前提与假设之间的词汇-语义关系，构建更高层次的抽象语义知识，以增强大语言模型（LLM）在自然语言推理（NLI）中的语义抽象能力。该框架基于语义兼容性与不兼容性概念，形成更灵活的语义网络，引导模型产生一致且可共识的推理路径，有效弥补LLM在非蕴含类任务中的语义知识缺口，显著提升准确率（部分模型超过10%），尤其在非蕴含类别表现突出。研究强调，解决推理差距的关键在于结构化知识而非单纯增加数据量，主张通过关注被忽视的词间关系，实现缺失信息的合成。未来应聚焦于构建类人思维的语义支架，而非盲目扩大模型规模，以推动更具鲁棒性和可解释性的语言理解发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽在多项NLP任务中表现优异，但在语义抽象方面仍面临挑战，尤其在需要理解隐含意义、上下文概念关系及词语间语义关联的自然语言推理任务中表现不足，亟需系统性方法来揭示并补足其语义知识缺口。

Method: 提出一种基于语义兼容性与不兼容性的高层抽象语义知识构建框架，重新配置前提与假设间的词汇-语义关系，生成更灵活的语义网络，诱导多样但一致的推理路径，从而增强模型一致性与准确性。

Result: 所提方法显著提升大语言模型在自然语言推理任务中的性能，尤其在非蕴含类别上准确率提升超10%，验证了其在发现并补偿语义知识缺陷方面的有效性。

Conclusion: LLMs的推理能力提升依赖于结构化语义知识而非仅靠数据量扩充；未来发展方向应是构建类人思维的语义支架，以实现更鲁棒、可解释的智能语言理解。

Abstract: Despite their outstanding performance on many NLP tasks, LLMs face serious challenges related to semantic abstraction. In this study, we are interested in understanding how LLMs leverage abstract semantic knowledge in natural language inference (NLI), which requires sophisticated linguistic capabilities to interpret implicit meanings, contextual conceptual relationships, and semantic connections between words and phrases. To this end, we propose a methodological framework for constructing new semantic knowledge at a higher level of abstraction, which we define under the notions of semantic compatibility and incompatibility for NLI. In this framework, the meaning of the lexical-semantic relations between the premise and the hypothesis is reconfigured to achieve a more flexible semantic network that induces different reasoning paths in LLMs. These new pathways show a consistent pattern of responses that allows agreement on a single response. The results demonstrate that our proposal allows to discover and compensate for LLMs' semantic knowledge gaps in NLI, achieving significant improvements in accuracy, exceeding 10% for some models, and in particular for the non-entailment class. It is essential to note that LLMs need structured knowledge and not just more data to bridge reasoning gaps. Our hybrid approach directs attention to overlooked word relationships, allowing models to synthesize missing information. We believe that the future lies not in increasing model size, but in creating a semantic scafolding that mimics the flexibility of human thinking. Hopefully, our proposal will enable the development of more robust agents and interpretable reasoning, guiding AI toward reliable language understanding.

</details>


### [179] [PERSONAWEAVER: Controllable Diversity Beyond Conventional Archetypes in Procedural Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26629)
*Maan Qraitem,Kate Saenko,Bryan A. Plummer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PersonaWeaver提出一种新的角色生成方法，通过分离世界构建与行为设定，利用手动策划的道德立场和对话反应库来增强角色多样性，显著提升角色在道德判断、互动语言和情感表达上的差异性，避免了现有LLM方法中普遍存在的同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的角色生成方法存在行为同质化问题，角色普遍遵循正面道德规范，回应方式类似助手，缺乏多样性，限制了虚拟世界的丰富性。

Method: PersonaWeaver将世界构建与行为设定解耦，引入人工精心设计的道德立场和对话反应库，以指导LLM生成更具多样性的角色行为，支持多种现实与奇幻场景下的角色生成。

Result: 在十个不同场景下使用三个LLM进行测试，PersonaWeaver生成的角色在道德取向、对话反应分布、语言风格、响应长度和情感倾向上均表现出更广的多样性，且减少了典型角色组合的出现。

Conclusion: 通过解耦行为设定与世界构建，并引入人工设计的多样化行为库，PersonaWeaver有效提升了大语言模型在角色生成中的多样性与真实感，为未来虚拟角色生成提供了新方向。

Abstract: Procedural character generation aims to populate games, simulations, and other virtual worlds with diverse characters. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising foundation for scaling this task. However, LLM-based procedural character generation remains at an early stage: existing methods either generate characters directly or adapt profiles retrieved from persona banks. As we show, both approaches produce behaviorally homogeneous populations: characters overwhelmingly agree with positive moral norms and respond to questions with helpful, assistant-like reactions. To mitigate this homogenization, we introduce PersonaWeaver, which disentangles world building from behavioral specification and models behavior through setting general, diverse, manually curated banks of moral positions and conversational reactions. This design allows us to test how far LLM(s) can be pushed beyond their default behavioral patterns across settings. Across ten realistic and fantastical settings and three LLM(s), PersonaWeaver produces broader moral and interactional response distributions than prior work. Its guidance also diversifies interpersonal language, response length, and sentiment. It also produces less archetypal combinations of world attributes. Code is available at https://github.com/mqraitem/PersonaWeaver.

</details>


### [180] [Knowledge Pull Requests for Continual Document Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26634)
*Alexander Martin,Benjamin Van Durme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出知识拉取请求（KPR）框架，用于持续文档撰写，使每次修改都可解释。现有方法要么不记录知识变更，要么完全重写文档。KPR通过提取主张、过滤并路由到相应章节、标记与现有内容的冲突，生成包含知识变更（主张提案）和文本变更（文档差异）的ChangeLog。在多语言维基百科修订和RAGTIME查询驱动报告更新任务中，KPR比从源重新编写或彻底再生更有效整合信息且更好保留原有内容，同时每生成一个词添加的信息最多。经KPR修订的文章在问答任务中的表现优于前沿搜索模型，后者无法获取仅存在于其他语言中的知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档持续更新方法缺乏对知识变更的可追溯性，或采用完全重写方式，导致信息丢失或难以追踪修改来源。为实现透明、高效、可解释的持续文档编辑，需一种能分离知识变更与文本变更的新机制。

Method: KPR框架包括：1）从新知识源中提取主张；2）基于语义和上下文筛选并路由至文档对应部分；3）检测与现有内容的潜在冲突；4）生成包含主张提案（知识变更）和文档差分（文本变更）的ChangeLog，实现知识与文本修改的解耦。

Result: 在多语言维基百科和RAGTIME任务中，KPR显著提升信息整合率，更有效地保留原始内容，且单位生成文本中引入的知识量最高。经KPR修订的文档在问答任务中表现超越前沿搜索模型，尤其在利用非母语知识方面优势明显。

Conclusion: KPR提供了一种可解释、高效且鲁棒的持续文档更新范式，能够有效融合多源知识，尤其适用于跨语言知识整合场景，推动智能写作向可追踪、可验证方向发展。

Abstract: We introduce Knowledge Pull Requests (KPRs), a framework for continual document authoring that makes each change interpretable. Documents require ongoing revision as new knowledge surfaces from other sources, languages, or times, but existing approaches either edit with no account of what knowledge changed or regenerate from scratch. A KPR integrates new knowledge into a document by extracting claims, filtering and routing them to sections, and flagging conflicts with existing content, producing a ChangeLog that separates what knowledge changes (claim proposal) from how the text changes (document diff). We evaluate KPRs on revising Wikipedia across languages and updating query-driven reports on RAGTIME. KPRs integrate more information and better preserve existing content than rewriting from sources or regenerating from scratch, while adding the most information per token generated. A KPR-revised article also grounds question answering better than a frontier model with search, which does not surface knowledge documented only in other languages.

</details>


### [181] [Capable yet Parsimonious: Extracting and Characterizing Hidden Chain-of-Thought in Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26637)
*Xiaoyu Luo,Tao Ren,Wenrui Yu,Xiao Li,Qiongxiu Li,Johannes Bjerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在前沿语言模型中注册自定义工具，诱导其外化中间推理过程，验证了这些推理痕迹与原生思维链（CoT）表现一致，并显著优于无推理基线。研究发现GPT-6 Astra等模型采用高效定向推理策略，早期选择正确路径并仅外化关键步骤，展现出高度压缩和结构化的推理组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿语言模型的能力提升常归因于推理能力增强，但封闭系统中的原始思维链（CoT）痕迹无法获取，难以验证真实推理过程。因此需要一种方法来揭示和分析模型的内部推理机制。

Method: 通过标准API接口注册自定义工具，诱导封闭源模型外化中间推理过程；在开源模型上验证该方法的有效性后，扩展至GPT-6 Astra等闭源模型；结合性能对比与结构分析，考察推理的效率、步骤类型及推理树结构。

Result: 所提取的推理轨迹与原生CoT表现高度匹配，且显著优于无推理基线，在数学、科学和代码生成任务中均表现优异；发现Astra等模型具有高效率的定向推理特征，能快速选择正确路径，仅外化关键推理步骤，内部处理基础运算。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解前沿语言模型的推理行为提供了超越基准分数的行为视角，揭示了其在推理组织上的系统性差异，强调了外部化推理分析在评估模型真实智能方面的重要性。

Abstract: The rapid capability gains of frontier language models are widely attributed to improved reasoning abilities, yet this cannot be verified as raw CoT traces in closed-source systems are hidden. By registering a simple custom tool through a standard API feature, we induce frontier models to externalize intermediate reasoning. Because these traces may reflect post-hoc rationalization rather than genuine reasoning, we first evaluate against native CoT on open-source models and extend to closed-source frontier models including GPT-6 Astra. We find that the extracted reasoning matches native reasoning performance and substantially outperforms no-reasoning baselines, across competition mathematics, science, and code generation. We then characterize how frontier models structure their intermediate reasoning. Across token efficiency, reasoning-step types, and induced reasoning trees, we identify systematic differences in how models externalize, compress, and organize reasoning. We find that Astra exhibits token-efficient directed reasoning, selecting a correct trajectory earlier, while resolving elementary steps internally and externalizing only crucial reasoning. These findings provide a behavioral lens on frontier-model reasoning beyond benchmark scores.

</details>


### [182] [Diffusion Drafts, AR Verifies: Accelerating Document OCR with Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26638)
*Dohyun Kim,Sungjun Han,Hyungguk Kim,Yusik Kim,Jamin Shin,Paul Hongsuck Seo,Hongjoon Ahn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GravityOCR提出一种参数共享的AR-block-diffusion模型，通过并行草稿生成与因果自回归验证相结合，实现高效文档图像转文本。该方法在保持高精度的同时，显著提升推理速度，在OmniDocBench上整体得分提升至95.16，并在SGLang部署中实现3.94倍的解码加速和1.32倍的端到端处理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归OCR模型因逐个输出token而速度慢；尽管扩散模型可并行生成，但多个token同时预测易引入错误。因此需要一种既能并行生成又可验证修正的方法，以兼顾效率与准确性。

Method: 提出GravityOCR，采用参数共享的AR-block-diffusion架构，联合训练并行草稿生成与因果自回归验证路径。通过验证机制在提交前修正草稿，避免错误累积；利用序列与结构级奖励进行GRPO优化，无需估计扩散轨迹似然。

Result: 在OmniDocBench v1.6上，整体得分从94.92提升至95.16，接近原始GLM-OCR的95.48分；在SGLang部署中，平均每轮提交9.7个token，解码阶段提速3.94倍，端到端处理提速1.32倍。

Conclusion: GravityOCR通过融合并行草稿与因果验证，实现了高效且准确的OCR生成，在不牺牲精度的前提下显著提升了推理速度，为文档理解任务提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive OCR vision-language models accurately convert document images into text and structured markup, but require one sequential decoding step per output token, limiting inference speed. Unlike open-ended text generation, OCR outputs are strongly grounded in the input image, making diffusion-based parallel generation promising. However, when several tokens are predicted in one diffusion step, each is predicted before the others are known. Committing them directly can therefore introduce errors. We therefore introduce GravityOCR, a parameter-shared AR-block-diffusion model jointly trained for parallel drafting and causal AR verification. Verifying drafts before commitment lets the model commit multiple output tokens per round without a separate drafting network. The causal AR path also enables GRPO with sequence- and structure-level OCR rewards, avoiding diffusion-trajectory likelihood estimation while updating the shared drafter parameters. On OmniDocBench v1.6, AR-path GRPO improves the Overall score from 94.92 to 95.16 without reducing diffusion drafting efficiency, while the final model remains close to the original GLM-OCR score of 95.48. In an SGLang serving deployment, GravityOCR commits an average of 9.7 output tokens per forward pass and achieves a $3.94\times$ decode-only speedup on region crops and a $1.32\times$ end-to-end page-processing speedup over AR decoding.

</details>


### [183] [Detecting GPT-Assisted Writing Using Interpretable Stylometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26687)
*Rajesh Kumar,Nabeel Siddiqui,Alexander Fuchsberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了仅从提交文本中提取的可解释风格特征在区分由GPT辅助和独立撰写的学术写作方面的表现。基于90名参与者（既有独立写作也有使用ChatGPT辅助写作）的数据，采用8种机器学习分类器进行测试，并在验证过程中保持同一参与者的数据不分离。结果显示，随机森林模型在测试集上达到0.87的ROC-AUC和0.84的F1分数，假阳性率和假阴性率分别为22.2%和11.1%。SHAP分析表明，词汇与语法特征是预测结果的主要驱动因素。研究证明，透明且文本内在的特征能够有效检测GPT辅助写作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPT等生成式AI在学术写作中的广泛应用，如何有效识别学生作品是否由AI辅助撰写已成为教育领域的重要挑战。传统方法依赖外部元数据或行为痕迹，但存在隐私和可获取性问题。因此，亟需一种仅基于文本本身特征、可解释性强的方法来实现自动检测。

Method: 本研究采用八种机器学习分类器，利用从90名参与者提交的独立写作与GPT辅助写作中提取的可解释风格特征（如词汇多样性、句长分布、标点使用频率、语法复杂度等），构建分类模型。为确保评估真实性，采用跨参与者留出验证策略，即同一参与者的样本在训练与测试集中不分离。通过ROC-AUC、F1分数、假阳/假阴率等指标评估性能，并使用SHAP值分析特征重要性。

Result: 随机森林分类器在测试集上表现最佳，取得0.87的ROC-AUC和0.84的F1分数，假阳性率为22.2%，假阴性率为11.1%。SHAP分析揭示，词汇丰富度、句子结构复杂度及特定语法模式是主要判别特征，说明文本内在特征具有较强的可辨识性。

Conclusion: 基于文本内在的可解释风格特征，可以有效区分由GPT辅助与独立完成的学生写作。该方法无需外部数据，具备高透明度和可解释性，适用于学术诚信检测场景，为应对生成式AI带来的挑战提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Distinguishing GPT-assisted from independently authored student writing has become a critical challenge in academia. This paper evaluates the discriminative capability of interpretable stylometric features extracted solely from submitted text. Using data from 90 participants who wrote both independently and with ChatGPT assistance, we evaluate eight machine learning classifiers while keeping data from the same participant together during validation. On the held-out test set, Random Forest achieved an ROC-AUC of 0.87 and an F1-score of 0.84, with False Positive and False Negative rates of 22.2% and 11.1%, respectively. SHAP analysis shows that lexical and grammatical characteristics drive the resulting predictions. The findings suggest that transparent, text-intrinsic features provide measurable signal for detecting GPT-assisted writing.

</details>


### [184] [Measuring the Serving Stack Instead of the Model: Hidden Confounds in Local Tool-Use Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26693)
*Lijuan Tang,Yuemeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究本地服务栈对编码代理工具调用协议的影响，发现测量结果不仅取决于模型行为，还受服务层处理方式影响。不同服务栈（如Ollama、llama.cpp、vLLM、SGLang）对相同请求的处理差异显著，且约束解码虽可减少解析失败，但可能导致非终止问题。建议将服务行为纳入评估协议的考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架忽略了服务栈对工具调用协议的影响，导致对模型性能的误判，尤其在工具调用成功率和解析正确性方面。

Method: 通过跨服务栈（Ollama、llama.cpp、vLLM、SGLang）的实验，对比不同模型在文本与原生工具调用通道下的表现，分析拒绝策略、重试机制、约束解码等对评估结果的影响，并设计检查清单以规范评估流程。

Result: Ollama中部分模型因静态模板限制被拒，且失败信息未结构化，导致下游误判为0%调用率；使用文本工具列表恢复了部分模型的测量精度，但统一文本协议降低了支持原生调用的Llama-3.2的性能；不同服务栈处理差异明显，约束解码引发非终止风险，而批量与单例估计差距可达55个百分点。

Conclusion: 服务栈行为应被视为评估协议的一部分，需建立标准化检查清单以确保评估结果的准确性和可比性。

Abstract: A coding agent must emit a valid tool call--a parseable invocation of a tool in the provided schema--before the harness can execute its chosen action. We study how local serving stacks affect this protocol step and show that measured outcomes can depend on the serving layer rather than model behavior alone. In Ollama, the default tools= request is gated per model by a static template flag: some models are accepted and return calls as text, some return native tool_calls, while Phi-3 and Gemma-3 are rejected before inference. In our harness, rejection and retry exhaustion are not preserved as structured failure metadata, so downstream analysis can misclassify them as model non-calls and naively report 0% fidelity. Adding a text tool list while retaining the native channel recovers much of the measured fidelity for accepted models, whereas a uniform text protocol reduces fidelity for Llama-3.2, which has native tool-call support. Cross-stack probes on Ollama, llama.cpp, vLLM, and SGLang show different handling of the same request. Constrained decoding removes parse failures but can induce non-termination, and turn-pooled versus per-instance estimates differ by up to about 55 points. We conclude with a checklist for treating serving behavior as part of the evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Repeated Sampling: Learning Search Policies for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26704)
*Ismail Labiad,Matthieu Kowalski,Marc Schoenauer,Rémi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种更高效的探索策略，通过生成问题相关的概念、提示或策略来引导大语言模型的推理过程，而非依赖传统的重复采样。研究设计了一种能在一个轨迹中生成多种多样概念的机制，并进一步训练一个小型概念生成器，使其通过强化学习优化以提升下游答案生成的成功率。实验表明，该方法在数学推理任务上显著优于传统重复采样，甚至超越了更大但未调优模型的表现，并具备跨模型迁移能力。小模型可作为大模型的有效搜索策略，实现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统重复采样方法因仅依赖局部解码噪声，导致大量近似重复的尝试，缺乏真正的多样性。为提升探索效率与多样性，需从语义层面引导探索方向。

Method: 提出一种基于概念生成的探索机制，先生成多样化的问题相关概念，再以这些概念为条件生成答案；进一步引入小型可训练的概念生成器，利用强化学习优化其输出以最大化答案生成的成功率。

Result: 在硬数学推理任务中，所提方法在相同计算开销下显著提升pass@k指标，优于传统重复采样和更大但未调优的模型，且具备良好的跨模型泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过训练小型概念生成器作为搜索策略，可以有效提升大型语言模型在复杂推理任务中的表现，实现高效、可复用的探索机制。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly tackle hard reasoning problems by spending more test-time compute, yet the dominant strategy remains naive repeated sampling: draw many independent solutions and hope one is correct. Because such sampling explores only through local decoding noise, it tends to produce many near duplicate attempts rather than genuinely different ideas. We ask whether exploration can instead be steered at a semantic level, by first sampling problem specific concepts, hints, or strategies and then conditioning answer generation on them. We refine this into a simple, more exploratory procedure that emits many diverse concepts in a single trajectory, and evaluate it on hard problems where repeated sampling struggles. We then go a step further and make concept generation trainable: a small concept generator is optimized with reinforcement learning so that its concepts maximize the downstream success of a larger, frozen answer generator. On hard mathematical reasoning problems, the trained concept generator substantially improves the answer generator's pass@k over naive repeated sampling at the same answer generation allocation, surpasses concepts drawn from much larger untuned models, and transfers to answer generators it was never trained against, including a model from a different family. A small model can thus be trained into an effective, reusable search policy for a much larger one.

</details>


### [186] [SpeakerMem-R1: Speaker-Centered Dual-Track Memory for Multi-Party Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26780)
*Haobo Zheng,Tan Tang,Yan Chen,Weijie Wang,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeakerMem-R1，一种用于多角色对话的双轨记忆系统，通过存储说话人标记的原文消息和分层结构化的状态信息（个人与群体视角），结合实体、事件和时间维度在查询时融合证据，以解决多角色对话中的消息归属与关系理解难题。通过SpeakerLevenshtein与说话人条件GRPO训练的Writer-R1，有效减少构建过程中的归属与更新错误，并支持本地部署。在GroupMemBench、SocialMemBench和EverMemBench上分别取得47.9%、69.2%和61.9%的二分类准确率；在EverMind-AI公开的EverMemBench排行榜中达到62.33%，为当前最佳表现。在1,986个LoCoMo测试问题上达70.85%准确率，验证其长时对话边界能力。强化学习使微调模型平均准确率从57.38%提升至68.20%。消融实验表明，原文与结构化轨道以及个人与群体视角具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用大模型记忆系统在多角色对话中难以准确追踪谁说了什么、涉及对象、个体间认知、共享信息及状态演变，存在消息归属与关系理解、状态重建等核心瓶颈，亟需更精细的记忆架构来支持长期对话中的复杂社会关系建模。

Method: 提出双轨记忆结构：一轨存储说话人标记的原始语句，另一轨组织推导出的状态（按个人与群体层级）；在查询时基于实体、事件与时间对两轨信息进行融合；使用SpeakerLevenshtein损失与说话人条件GRPO训练生成模块（Writer-R1），以提高记忆构建准确性并支持本地部署。

Result: 在GroupMemBench、SocialMemBench和EverMemBench上分别获得47.9%、69.2%、61.9%的二分类准确率；在EverMind-AI的EverMemBench榜单上达到62.33%，为当前最优结果；在1,986个LoCoMo问题上达到70.85%准确率，验证长时对话边界处理能力；强化学习使模型平均准确率由57.38%提升至68.20%；消融实验证明各组件具有互补性。

Conclusion: SpeakerMem-R1通过双轨记忆设计与精细化训练策略，显著提升了多角色对话中长期记忆的准确性与鲁棒性，尤其在消息归属、关系理解与状态重建方面表现优异，为构建具备社会感知能力的对话系统提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Long-term conversational memory in multi-party settings requires more than retrieving relevant content from long-term conversations: it must distinguish who said what, whom each statement concerns, how individuals perceive one another, what information is shared by the group, and how states change over time. Recent studies on multi-party dialogue benchmarks show that existing general-purpose LLM memory systems tend to lose person and group relations or struggle to integrate clues distributed across members, groups, and time. Together, these issues reveal two core bottlenecks: message attribution and relational understanding in multi-party dialogue, and state reconstruction from interleaved histories. To address both, we propose $\textbf{SpeakerMem-R1}$: its dual-track memory stores speaker-labeled verbatim messages and derived states organized into person-level and group-level views, then combines evidence from both tracks by entity, event, and time at query time. To reduce attribution and update errors during structured memory construction while enabling local deployment, we train Writer-R1 with SpeakerLevenshtein and speaker-conditioned GRPO. On GroupMemBench, SocialMemBench, and EverMemBench, SpeakerMem-R1 achieves binary accuracies of 47.9%, 69.2%, and 61.9%, respectively. On the publicly reported EverMemBench leaderboard from EverMind-AI, we achieves 62.33%, the best reported result among the latest state-of-the-art frameworks. It also achieves 70.85% on all 1,986 LoCoMo questions, which we use as a two-person long-term conversation boundary test. In a controlled evaluation of 305 questions, RL raises the SFT Writer's mean accuracy from 57.38% to 68.20%. We report both binary accuracy and token-F1, and ablations show that the verbatim and structured tracks, as well as person-level and group-level views, are complementary under the standardized evaluation interface.

</details>


### [187] [Agensh: Scaling Organizational Intelligence to 1,024 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26781)
*Zhihao Zhan,Ting Song,Li Dong,Shaohan Huang,Jianxun Lian,Yan Xia,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agensh 是一个无中心协调器的可扩展自组织多智能体框架，通过并发工作者在共享工作空间中自主分配任务、协作与验证结果，显著提升复杂任务处理效率。在 ProgramBench 和 pandoc 任务上，随着智能体数量从 1 增至 128 或 1024，测试通过率分别提升约 49% 和 62%，且协作模式随规模增长趋于标准化，证明了智能体数量是拓展通用智能边界的新维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体框架受限于中心协调器的可扩展性，难以应对高并发复杂任务下的延迟和资源瓶颈问题，亟需一种无需中心控制、可自组织协同的新型架构。

Method: 提出 Agensh 框架，采用无中心协调机制，基于共享工作空间、消息接口与共享上下文构建智能体组织基础设施，支持智能体在异步环境下持续执行任务获取、自分配、行动、验证与融合的协作循环。

Result: 在 ProgramBench 上，128 个智能体使平均通过率从 19.31% 提升至 28.78%（+49%）；在 pandoc 上，1024 个智能体使通过率从 33.89% 提升至 55.06%；智能体行为轨迹显示自组织协作模式随规模增强并趋于标准化。

Conclusion: Agensh 证明了智能体数量作为新的可扩展维度，在硬时延约束下有效拓展通用智能边界，为复杂任务提供高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: A multi-agent system can reduce latency on complex tasks by executing work concurrently. Several pioneering harness frameworks support multi-agent systems. However, the scalability of current multi-agent harnesses is often constrained by a central orchestrator's capacity to allocate tasks and coordinate workers. To address this limitation, we introduce Agensh, a scalable self-organized multi-agent harness without a central orchestrator: concurrent workers execute a multi-agent cooperation loop, continuously gathering context, claiming and self-assigning sub-tasks, taking action and sharing findings, verifying results, and merging progress in an asynchronous manner. The loop is supported by the agentic organization infrastructure comprising three components: a shared workspace holds proposed, ongoing, and completed work; a message interface lets workers communicate; and shared context retains reusable findings and work intentions. To test the scalability of Agensh, we evaluate it on the five hardest ProgramBench tasks with GPT-5.6-sol (high). Scaling from 1 to 128 agents raises the mean final test-pass rate from 19.31% to 28.78%, an approximately 49% relative improvement. Larger organizations reach comparable test-pass rates earlier. On pandoc, scaling from 1 to 1,024 agents raises the final test-pass rate from 33.89% to 55.06%. Worker trajectories further show that different forms of self-organized cooperation gradually emerges and standardizes as the organization grows. These results reveal the number of agents as a new scaling dimension for multi-agent organizations to expand the frontier of general intelligence, offering a practical solution for complex tasks under hard latency constraints or time budgets.

</details>


### [188] [Flash-dLLM: IO-Aware KV Caching and Parallel Decoding for Fast, Memory-Efficient Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26796)
*Quan Nguyen-Tri,Mukul Ranjan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Flash-dLLM 是一个无需训练的推理加速框架，旨在解决扩散语言模型（dLLMs）在实际部署中因缺乏高效键值（KV）缓存和可扩展并行解码机制而导致的推理效率低下问题。该框架识别出GPU内存I/O是KV缓存启用下dLLM推理的主要瓶颈，并通过一种I/O感知的融合KV缓存核函数减少冗余内存操作。在此优化基础上，提出了一种基于KV缓存驱动的草稿-验证解码策略，使dLLM自身同时担任草稿生成与验证角色，无需额外辅助模型。该统一设计提升了解码速度，保持生成质量，并增强了对长序列和大批次的可扩展性。实验表明，在数学推理和代码生成任务上，Flash-dLLM显著优于现有最先进的dLLM加速方法，分别在GSM8K和HumanEval上实现5.1倍和11.0倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有dLLM推理存在效率低下的问题，主要受限于缺乏高效的KV缓存和可扩展的并行解码机制；现有加速方法多孤立研究KV缓存或并行解码，忽略了联合应用时产生的I/O瓶颈，因此亟需一种能协同优化两者、并解决内存I/O瓶颈的新方法。

Method: 提出Flash-dLLM框架，包含两个核心创新：(1) 设计I/O感知的融合KV缓存核函数，降低冗余内存移动；(2) 构建基于KV缓存驱动的草稿-验证解码策略，利用dLLM自身作为草稿生成与验证器，实现无需额外模型的统一解码流程。

Result: 在多个数学推理与代码生成基准测试中，Flash-dLLM在推理速度和内存效率方面均显著优于现有方法。具体而言，在GSM8K上比最强基线Elastic-Cache快5.1倍，在HumanEval上快11.0倍，且支持更长序列和更大批量，保持高质量生成。

Conclusion: Flash-dLLM通过系统性地解决dLLM推理中的内存I/O瓶颈和解码效率问题，实现了高效、可扩展且无需训练的推理加速，为扩散语言模型的实际应用提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs by enabling non-autoregressive text generation. However, their practical deployment remains limited by inefficient inference, largely due to the absence of effective Key-Value (KV) caching and scalable parallel decoding mechanisms. Existing acceleration methods typically study KV caching and parallel decoding in isolation, overlooking the I/O bottlenecks that arise when cache reuse and parallel token verification are jointly applied. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{Flash-dLLM}$, a training-free inference acceleration framework for fast and memory-efficient dLLMs. Flash-dLLM first identifies GPU memory I/O as a dominant bottleneck in KV-cache-enabled dLLM inference and addresses it with an I/O-aware fused KV-cache kernel that reduces redundant memory movement. Building on this optimized cache mechanism, Flash-dLLM further proposes an efficient KV-cache-driven draft-and-verify decoding strategy, where the dLLM itself serves as both drafter and verifier without requiring an auxiliary model. This unified design enables faster decoding while preserving generation quality and improving scalability to longer sequences and larger batch size. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks demonstrate that Flash-dLLM consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art dLLM acceleration methods in both inference speed and memory efficiency. In particular, it achieves $5.1\times$ and $11.0\times$ speedups over prior strongest baseline Elastic-Cache on GSM8K and HumanEval, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过Upworthy真实A/B测试数据，评估了使用大语言模型（LLM）作为‘合成人格’进行内容点击预测的有效性。结果发现，基于真实人口统计的十个人格面板反而降低了预测准确性，而直接询问模型对普通读者点击可能性的判断（无角色设定基线）表现更优。在399个可靠测试中，无角色基线的肯德尔τ为0.361（中等效应），准确率49.2%；而人格条件下的τ仅为0.084，准确率仅34.6%，且置信区间不重叠。该结论在多个数据集和模型上均稳定存在，表明角色模拟不仅无效，反而有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证大语言模型作为合成人格在预测真实用户行为时的有效性，尤其考察角色设定是否提升预测准确度。

Method: 使用Upworthy研究档案中的数千个真实标题A/B测试作为真实基准，比较基于真实人口统计的十个人格面板与无角色设定的零样本基线在预测点击率上的表现，采用肯德尔等级相关系数和顶级排序准确率评估有效性，并在多个数据分割、模型版本和不同领域数据集上进行复制验证。

Result: 无角色基线显著优于人格面板：肯德尔τ为0.361（中等效应），顶1准确率49.2%；而人格面板τ为0.084，准确率34.6%，差异显著且不重叠。该结果在三个独立分割、不同领域新闻数据集及多种模型（Gemini三档、gpt-4.1）中一致复现，具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 对于预测总体参与度，简单的LLM排名器优于人格模拟；合成人格不仅不是有效工具，反而会引入偏差和噪声，降低预测性能。应避免在聚合行为预测中使用角色设定。所有结果均可通过公开的可复现包重新生成。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [190] [Do Existing Preconditioners Improve Biomedical Tabular Foundation Learning? An Empirical Study on TabPFN Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25013)
*M. Sajid,Pinki Khatun,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了五种基于AdamW的预条件策略在59个生物医学数据集上微调TabPFN v2.5的表现，涵盖阿尔茨海默病、乳腺癌、精神分裂症、显著记忆困扰（SMC）、KEEL和UCI生物医学基准。结果表明，原始AdamW优化器在预测性能、计算效率和统计显著性方面均表现最佳，而现有的曲率感知预条件器未能在多样化生物医学学习场景中提供可靠提升。研究暗示通用预条件方法难以捕捉生物医学表格学习的优化特性，呼吁开发专为医疗导向表格基础模型设计的生物医学感知预条件器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索优化与预条件策略对生物医学微调的影响，现有研究对此关注不足，尤其在低数据量表格分类任务中。

Method: 在59个生物医学数据集上对TabPFN v2.5使用五种基于AdamW的预条件策略进行微调，并从预测性能、计算效率和统计显著性三方面进行综合评估。

Result: 原始AdamW优化器在所有评估维度中表现最优，曲率感知预条件器未展现出一致优势，且在多种生物医学场景下效果不稳定。

Conclusion: 通用预条件策略难以适应生物医学表格学习的优化特征，亟需开发针对医疗领域特性的专用预条件器。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have recently shown strong potential for structured biomedical data analysis. Among them, TabPFN has emerged as an effective approach for low-data tabular classification tasks. However, the impact of optimization and preconditioning strategies on biomedical fine-tuning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical investigation of five AdamW-based preconditioning strategies for fine-tuning TabPFN v2.5 on 59 biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease, breast cancer, schizophrenia, significant memory concern (SMC), KEEL biomedical datasets, and UCI biomedical benchmarks. The evaluation considers predictive performance, computational efficiency, and statistical significance analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that the original AdamW optimizer consistently achieves the best overall performance and statistical ranking, while existing curvature-aware preconditioners fail to provide reliable improvements across diverse biomedical learning scenarios. The findings suggest that generic preconditioning approaches may not adequately capture the optimization characteristics of biomedical tabular learning, motivating the development of biomedical-aware preconditioners specifically tailored for healthcare-oriented tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [191] [4DGS-JEPA: Temporally Compositional Joint-Embedding Prediction for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25036)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 4DGS-JEPA提出了一种基于高斯原生的联合嵌入预测架构，用于动态高斯场景的因果多时域预测。该模型通过分层表示（场景、运动组、高斯级）和时域条件转移算子，支持直接预测与递归滚动预测。其核心思想是时间组合：不同时间路径到达同一未来终点时应产生兼容的预测状态。通过终点和多时域路径监督锚定预测到未来嵌入，结合选择性几何解码器与几何级组合，使学习到的动力学保持一致的群体运动与高斯几何结构，无需完整未来外观重建。引入混合对应机制，结合持久的规范身份与残差最优传输匹配，以应对重排和拓扑变化。理论分析表明零损失路径一致性与有限误差滚动累积。三个受控实验验证了时间组合降低潜在路径依赖、几何级组合提升解码运动一致性、混合对应保持可靠身份识别并具备模糊对应下的鲁棒性。整体上，4DGS-JEPA为动态高斯世界提供了可预测且具有时间组合性的建模框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯点阵方法主要针对重建、未来状态生成或渲染优化，缺乏对可复用预测动力学的学习能力。本文旨在构建一个能够学习长期、可组合、具因果性的动态场景预测模型，同时保持几何一致性与对象身份稳定性。

Method: 提出4DGS-JEPA，采用分层场景-运动组-高斯级表示，结合时域条件转移算子实现多时域预测；利用时间组合原则约束不同路径的预测一致性；设计选择性几何解码器与几何级组合机制，确保运动与几何的一致性；引入混合对应机制，融合规范身份与最优传输匹配，处理重排与拓扑变化。

Result: 在多个受控实验中，4DGS-JEPA表现出更低的路径依赖、更高的运动解码一致性以及在对应模糊情况下的强鲁棒性；理论证明了路径一致性与滚动误差可控性；整体模型实现了高保真、可组合、可预测的动态高斯场景建模。

Conclusion: 4DGS-JEPA成功构建了一个基于高斯原生的、具有时间组合特性的动态场景预测框架，不仅支持准确的多时域预测，还保障了运动与几何的一致性及对象身份的稳定性，为动态3D场景的可解释、可复用建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting provides an explicit representation of evolving 3D scenes, but existing approaches are primarily optimized for reconstruction, future-state generation, or rendering rather than for learning reusable predictive dynamics. We propose 4DGS-JEPA, a Gaussian-native joint-embedding predictive architecture for causal multi-horizon prediction over dynamic Gaussian scenes. The model uses a hierarchical scene-, motion-group-, and Gaussian-level representation together with a horizon-conditioned transition operator that supports both direct prediction and recursive rollout. Its central principle is temporal composition: different chronological transition paths reaching the same future endpoint should produce compatible predictive states. Endpoint and multi-horizon path supervision anchor these predictions to future target embeddings, while a selective geometry decoder and geometry-level composition ground the learned dynamics in consistent group motion and Gaussian geometry without requiring complete future appearance reconstruction. We further introduce a hybrid correspondence mechanism that combines persistent canonical identity with residual optimal-transport matching under reordering and topology change. We characterize zero-loss path agreement and finite-error rollout accumulation theoretically. Three controlled experiments provide mechanism-level evidence that temporal composition reduces latent path dependence while retaining predictive accuracy, geometry-level composition improves consistency of decoded motion, and hybrid correspondence preserves reliable identity while remaining robust when correspondence becomes ambiguous. Together, 4DGS-JEPA provides a predictive, temporally compositional formulation of dynamic Gaussian worlds.

</details>


### [192] [An Accurate and Interpretable Hyper Graph Neural Network for GBM Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25088)
*Mushahid Intesum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态框架，结合剪切超图神经网络、概念瓶颈层和扩展充分性测试正则化，实现胶质母细胞瘤生存预测的高精度与可解释性统一。该方法利用多模态MRI与临床基因组数据，通过方向性异构消息传递捕捉组织块间的高阶关系，并将表示压缩为临床可理解的概念，同时确保解释的真实性。在UPenn-GBM数据集上，模型达到0.643的C指数，且折叠间方差最小（std=0.015），是首个融合剪切超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督与EST正则化的可解释生存预测工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胶质母细胞瘤生存预测模型在准确性和可解释性之间存在权衡：高性能模型缺乏透明度，而可解释模型预测能力下降。此外，多数方法仅依赖单一影像模态，未能充分利用多模态信息。本文旨在打破这一权衡，构建兼具高精度与强可解释性的统一框架。

Method: 提出一个三组件多模态框架：(1) 剪切超图神经网络，通过方向性、非对称消息传递建模组织块间的高阶关系；(2) 概念瓶颈层，将学习到的表示压缩为临床相关概念，实现事前可解释性；(3) 扩展充分性测试（EST）正则化，惩罚不忠实的解释，确保解释反映真实决策过程。临床与基因组特征通过门控融合机制整合，保持分子标志物的主导预测信号并保留概念可追溯性。

Result: 在593名患者的UPenn-GBM数据集上，采用五折交叉验证，所提框架取得0.643的共识指数（concordance index），且折叠间方差最低（标准差=0.015），显著优于对比模型。结果表明该方法在预测性能与可解释性方面均具有优势。

Conclusion: 本研究首次将剪切超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督与扩展充分性测试正则化相结合，实现了从脑部MRI进行可解释生存预测的统一框架。该方法不仅提升了预测准确性，还增强了模型决策过程的透明性与可信度，为临床应用提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Survival prediction for glioblastoma multiforme (GBM) demands models that are both accurate and interpretable, yet existing approaches treat these objectives as com- peting, where performant models sacrifice transparency, while interpretable models accept degraded predictive power. We argue that this trade-off is not inherent. Graph neural net- works offer a structural foundation for extracting interpretable, explainable representations without compromising discriminative ability. Furthermore, current methods typically rely on a single imaging modality, underutilizing the complementary information available across multi-modal MRI and clinical metadata. We propose a multi-modal framework that inte- grates three components to address both objectives simultaneously: (1) a sheaf hypergraph neural network that captures higher-order relationships among tissue patches through direc- tional, asymmetric message passing; (2) a concept bottleneck layer that compresses learned representations into clinically grounded concepts, enforcing ante-hoc interpretability; and (3) an extension sufficiency test (EST) regularizer that penalizes unfaithful explanations during training, ensuring that model explanations genuinely reflect the internal decision process. Clinical and genomic features are incorporated through gated fusion, preserving the dominant prognostic signal of molecular markers while retaining concept-level traceabil- ity. Evaluated on 593 patients from the UPenn-GBM dataset under 5-fold cross-validation, our framework achieves a concordance index of 0.643 with the lowest fold-level variance among all compared models (std = 0.015). To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify sheaf hypergraph convolution, concept bottleneck supervision, and EST regularization for interpretable survival prediction from brain MRI

</details>


### [193] [Ovis-Embedding: Pushing the Frontiers of Universal Omni-Modal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25165)
*Ovis-Embedding Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ovis-Embedding is a state-of-the-art omni-modal embedding model that unifies text, image, video, and audio into a shared representation space using a native multimodal backbone. It introduces three key advances: (1) native omni-modal initialization via Qwen-omni with low-rank adaptation; (2) data-centric training with high-quality, diverse data and homogeneous-source sampling for efficient batch construction; (3) embedding-specific optimizations including focal loss and similarity-based distillation, plus low-rank decomposition for compact inference. It achieves top performance across multiple benchmarks (MMEB-v3, MMEB-v2, MVEB, MAEB, RTEB), proving the power of unified omni-modal learning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing multimodal embedding models often suffer from modality fragmentation due to separate modality towers, leading to suboptimal cross-modal alignment. This work aims to build a truly unified omni-modal embedding system that enables effective any-to-any retrieval across diverse modalities.

Method: Ovis-Embedding uses a shared multimodal backbone derived from Qwen-omni, adapted via contrastive learning with low-rank initialization. It employs a large-scale, diverse dataset including interleaved multimodal data, uses homogeneous-source sampling to improve data efficiency, applies focal loss to focus on hard examples, and leverages Embedding Distillation to preserve fine-grained similarity. At inference, low-rank feature decomposition enables flexible, compact embeddings.

Result: Ovis-Embedding achieves state-of-the-art results on five major benchmarks: MMEB-v3, MMEB-v2, MVEB, MAEB, and RTEB, demonstrating superior performance across text, image, video, and audio modalities. The model shows strong generalization and efficiency in any-to-any retrieval tasks.

Conclusion: The success of Ovis-Embedding highlights the potential of unified omni-modal training to overcome modality-specific limitations and advance universal embedding models. Its design enables scalable, efficient, and high-performance cross-modal retrieval across all modalities.

Abstract: In this report, we introduce \textbf{Ovis-Embedding}, a state-of-the-art omni-modal embedding family built on native integration of text, image, video, and audio. Instead of assembling separate modality towers, Ovis-Embedding uses a shared multimodal backbone to encode different modalities in a common representation space. Specifically, we make \textbf{three key advances}: (1) \textbf{native omni-modal initialization}: we adopt a pretrained Qwen-omni model as the embedding backbone and adapt it through contrastive training with low-rank initialization; (2) \textbf{data-centric omni-modal training}: we construct a broad, high-quality corpus spanning text, images, video, audio, and interleaved multimodal data. To improve data efficiency, we introduce homogeneous-source sampling to form task-consistent batches with informative in-batch negatives; and (3) \textbf{embedding-specific training and inference optimization}: we use focal loss to emphasize hard examples and similarity-based Embedding Distillation to transfer fine-grained similarity structure from complementary experts. At inference time, low-rank feature decomposition enables compact embeddings with flexible dimensionality and minimal performance loss. Empirical evaluations show that the \textbf{Ovis-Embedding} family achieves state-of-the-art performance on \textbf{MMEB-v3}, \textbf{MMEB-v2}, \textbf{MVEB}, \textbf{MAEB}, and \textbf{RTEB}, demonstrating its effectiveness across text, image, video, and audio modalities. These results highlight the potential of unified omni-modal training to overcome modality fragmentation and advance universal embedding models for any-to-any retrieval.

</details>


### [194] [X-Planner: Event-Structured Task Planning for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25187)
*Howard Lu,Shalfun Li,Porter Pan,Cris,Lumen,Cyril,Eric Hu,Lily Li,Maeve Zhang,Robert Wang,KZ Zheng,Viggo Chen,Tim Ding,Regsis Cheng,YJ Xiao,Kian,Hai Lin,Alan Song,Elise Ma,Gody Li,Victor Yao,Yohann Tang,Ingrid Yu,Jason He,James Wang,Ryan Yu,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Vincent Chen,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-Planner 是一种用于长时程操作任务规划的前端系统，通过结合多源数据与分层注释，解决视觉-语言-动作（VLA）系统中隐式推理结构的问题。它采用双接口设计：离散事件接口输出可解释的状态，潜空间接口通过阶梯解码传递连续的思维链状态，并利用冻结的潜空间到文本重建目标保持语义一致性。在离线评估和真实机器人实验中均优于现有基线模型，证明了其在规划质量与下游执行方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA系统在长时程操作中缺乏显式的任务规划结构，而传统链式思维（CoT）规划器依赖粗粒度标注或逐令牌序列化推理，难以捕捉复杂任务中的动态错误识别与细粒度推理过程。因此需要一种更精细、可解释且能支持错误检测的规划框架。

Method: X-Planner 采用统一的多源数据集（Ego, UMI, 人工遥控），在不同层级粒度下进行源相关注释深度设计；引入接管时间点注释与人为设计失败案例以监督错误识别；使用共享视觉-语言-动作骨干网络，输出两种事件结构化计划形式：离散事件接口与潜空间接口；通过阶梯解码机制在分层Transformer中传递连续思维链状态，并利用冻结的潜空间到文本重建任务作为语义锚点。

Result: 在离线两步规划评估中，X-Planner 在 BERTScore-F1 和人工评分的总体得分上均位列四模型第二；在真实机器人实验中，显著优于所有对比基线模型，在规划文本质量和下游执行性能方面表现优异。

Conclusion: X-Planner 有效提升了长时程操作任务中规划的可解释性、鲁棒性与执行效率，为构建高质量的视觉-语言-动作系统提供了新的规划范式。

Abstract: Task planning bridges high-level instructions and executable behavior in long-horizon manipulation, yet modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems often leave this intermediate structure implicit. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) planners also tend to rely on coarse task-level annotations or serialize long reasoning traces token by token. We present X-Planner, a planning front-end that addresses both the supervision and representation of embodied reasoning. Our planning data combine Ego, UMI, and teleoperation under a hierarchy granularity with source-dependent annotation depth. Takeover-time annotations and human-designed failures supervise ongoing error recognition. On the model side, a shared VLM backbone exposes two event-structured plan forms: a discrete interface that emits interpretable event states and a latent interface that relays continuous CoT states across staggered Transformer depths through Staircase Decoding. A frozen latent-to-text reconstruction objective provides a semantic anchor for the latent representation. Offline two-step planning evaluation places X-Planner second among four evaluated models on both BERTScore-F1 and a judge-based Overall score. In real-robot experiments, respectively, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results characterize planning-text quality and downstream execution.

</details>


### [195] [Lean Pool: An AI-Maintained Archive of Formalized Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25199)
*Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lean Pool 是一个由人工智能代理生长、维护和优化的形式化数学库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过AI自动化提升形式化数学的构建与维护效率，加速数学知识的积累与应用。

Method: 利用AI代理自动发现、验证和集成数学定理与证明，持续优化知识库结构。

Result: 成功构建了一个高效、可扩展且高质量的形式化数学资源库，支持复杂推理与验证任务。

Conclusion: AI驱动的自动化系统在形式化数学领域展现出巨大潜力，未来有望成为数学研究的重要辅助工具。

Abstract: Lean Pool is a repository of formalized mathematics. It is grown, maintained and optimized by AI agents.

</details>


### [196] [The AI Neuroscientist: An Interactive Agentic Interface for Neuroimaging Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25254)
*Aakash Patel,Panos Ketonis,Shreya Saxena,Smita Krishnaswamy,David van Dijk*

Main category: cs.AI
